### [1] [CHEX-MATE: Density profile reconstruction in galaxy clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05476)
*I. Bartalucci,G. W. Pratt,E. Rasia,M. Arnaud,R. T. Duffy,Y. E. Bahar,M. Balboni,E. Baraldi,R. M. Batalha,A. Bonafede,H. Bourdin,F. De Luca,M. De Petris,S. Ettori,A. Ferragamo,M. Gaspari,F. Gastaldello,M. Gitti,A. Iqbal,M. Lepore,S. T. Kay,L. Lovisari,P. Mazzotta,B. J. Maughan,F. Oppizzi,E. Pointecouteau,M. Rossetti,J. Sayers,M. Sereno*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本研究在CHEX-MATE项目框架下，利用109个模拟星系团沿40条视线方向的分析，比较了方位角平均与中位数去投影方法对星系团内介质（ICM）径向密度分布重建的影响；结果表明中位数法在[0.15–1.3]R₅₀₀范围内几乎无偏，而平均法易受子结构干扰，尤其在动力学扰动系统中偏差显著；均值/中位数比可统计反映投影气体不均匀性，但个体星系团适用性受限；二者互补使用是大样本X射线星系团分析的最佳策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估不同去投影方法（方位角平均 vs 中位数）在重建星系团内介质（ICM）密度剖面时对形态、动力学状态及方法选择的敏感性，以提升X射线观测数据的可靠性。

Method: 基于CHEX-MATE项目中的109个模拟星系团，沿40条独立视线方向进行分析；对比方位角平均与中位数两种去投影技术，并考察子结构掩模效果及扰动系统影响。

Result: 方位角中位数法在r∈[0.15–1.3]R₅₀₀范围内恢复真实气体密度几乎无偏；平均法受子结构显著影响，即使掩模后残余偏差仍可达<5%，在扰动系统或子结构未分辨时偏差剧增；均值/中位数比可统计反映投影不均匀性，但个体应用受限于内在散射和投影效应。

Conclusion: 方位角平均与中位数剖面具有互补性：中位数提供稳健无偏基准，平均值携带子结构信息；联合使用是处理大样本X射线星系团数据最全面、可靠的方法。

Abstract: We investigated the reconstruction of the intra-cluster medium radial density profiles in galaxy clusters, focusing on the impact of cluster morphology, dynamical state, and methodological choices, within the framework of the CHEX-MATE project. Using a sample of 109 simulated galaxy clusters analysed along 40 independent lines of sight, we compared azimuthal mean and median techniques for deprojection. We find that azimuthal median-based density profiles recover the true gas density with negligible bias over the radial range $[0.15-1.3] R_{500}$. Mean based profiles are affected by the presence of substructures. While an accurate masking of clumps or substructures can reduce the bias to $< 5\%$, the discrepancy increases substantially in disturbed systems or when substructures remain unresolved. The mean-to-median density ratio statistically traces projected gas inhomogeneities, although intrinsic scatter and projection effects limit its use for individual clusters. The complementary use of mean and median profiles therefore provides the most complete approach for large X-ray cluster samples.

</details>


### [2] [Gravitational waves flooding the Universe after wall domination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05504)
*Sabir Ramazanov,Zhen-Min Zeng*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种早期宇宙场景，其中辐射主导时期被域壁主导时期短暂中断，域壁随后湮灭为粒子；该过程产生具有独特谱形的可观测引力波背景，并可能影响中微子有效数目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索域壁主导时期对早期宇宙演化及引力波背景产生的新机制，特别是与传统次主导域壁模型的区别及其可观测性。

Method: 理论建模与分析：构建域壁主导—湮灭的宇宙学演化框架，计算其产生的张量扰动（引力波）的产生、冻结、再进入过程及能谱特征。

Result: 预测了区别于传统模型的独特引力波谱形；该引力波背景强度足够被当前及未来探测器在宽频段内探测；其能量密度足以显著影响有效中微子数目（N_eff），从而对域壁主导时长施加观测约束。

Conclusion: 域壁短暂主导时期是一种可行且可观测的早期宇宙机制，其产生的引力波为检验高能物理和宇宙学提供了新探针。

Abstract: We discuss a possible early Universe scenario, where radiation era is interrupted by the epoch of domination of domain walls eventually annihilating into particles. During this period the Universe undergoes accelerated expansion, while the comoving correlation length of the domain wall network remains constant. The network serves as a source of tensor modes, which get frozen upon exiting the horizon similarly to the case of inflationary fluctuations. The modes re-enter the horizon during the subsequent stage of radiation domination and since then contribute to the gravitational wave background. The spectral shape of gravitational waves predicted in this scenario is markedly different compared to the case of domain walls remaining sub-dominant all the way till annihilation. These gravitational waves are strong enough to be observed with present-day and future facilities in a broad range of frequencies. In particular, their energy density is sufficient to produce a significant impact on the effective number of neutrino species, unless the duration of wall domination is suitably constrained.

</details>


### [3] [An obstruction to cosmic acceleration from shift-symmetric dark sector interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05560)
*Jose Beltrán Jiménez,Luis J. Garay,Erik Jensko,María Pérez Garrote*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用宇宙学介质的有效场理论，证明暗物质与暗能量之间的导数相互作用无法同时支持慢滚暴胀并避免不稳定性；稳定性条件还排除了幽灵态到非幽灵态的穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质与暗能量之间导数相互作用对宇宙学演化（特别是慢滚暴胀和状态方程穿越）的理论可行性与稳定性约束。

Method: 基于宇宙学介质的有效场理论，分析暗流体（完美流体+超流体）的导数相互作用，并将暗物质与暗能量作为有效组分进行分解，推导稳定性条件。

Result: 导数相互作用无法在维持慢滚暴胀的同时避免不稳定性；稳定性条件禁止幽灵态（w < -1）向非幽灵态（w ≥ -1）的状态方程穿越。

Conclusion: 暗部门模型的构造必须严格满足稳定性要求，这从根本上限制了其可能的宇宙学动力学行为。

Abstract: Using the effective field theory of cosmological media, we show that derivative interactions in the dark sector between a perfect fluid and a superfluid cannot support slow-roll expansion while remaining free from instabilities. Furthermore, decomposing the dark sector medium into effective dark matter and dark energy components, we find that the stability conditions exclude a phantom-to-non-phantom crossing. Our approach gives a clear route for constructing theoretically viable dark sector models, and highlights the fundamental constraints that stability requirements impose on their cosmological dynamics.

</details>


### [4] [PrimordialPy: A Python library for single-field inflation, primordial black holes, and scalar-induced gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05566)
*Flavio Pineda*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: PrimordialPy是一个开源Python库，用于计算任意规范单场暴胀模型的宇宙学预测，包括原初功率谱、原初黑洞质量分数与丰度，以及标量诱导引力波谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为早期宇宙学研究和教育提供一个模块化、高效且易于使用的工具，以支持任意规范单场暴胀模型的数值计算与预测。

Method: 通过数值求解暴胀背景动力学和曲率扰动方程，计算标量与张量扰动的原初功率谱，并自动推导原初黑洞质量分数、丰度及标量诱导引力波谱。用户只需输入标量场势函数V(φ)及其参数即可使用。

Result: 实现了对任意规范单场暴胀模型的完整宇宙学预测能力，涵盖原初功率谱、PBH丰度与质量分布、SIGW谱；具备良好模块性、效率与可访问性，并配有完整文档、验证基准与示例。

Conclusion: PrimordialPy是一个功能完备、易用性强的开源工具，显著提升了暴胀模型理论预测的便捷性与可靠性，适用于科研与教学。

Abstract: We present \texttt{PrimordialPy}, an open-source Python library designed to compute the cosmological predictions of arbitrary canonical single-field inflationary models. By numerically solving the background dynamics and the curvature perturbation equation, the package evaluates the primordial power spectrum of scalar and tensor perturbations at all relevant scales. Furthermore, it automatically computes the resulting mass fraction and abundance of Primordial Black Holes (PBHs), as well as the associated spectrum of scalar-induced gravitational waves (SIGWs). Users can seamlessly implement any canonical single-field model by simply specifying the inflaton potential $V(φ)$ and its parameters. Designed to be modular, efficient, and highly accessible, \texttt{PrimordialPy} comes with comprehensive documentation, validation benchmarks, and practical examples, making it a versatile tool for both research and education in early-universe cosmology.

</details>


### [5] [Observational Test of the Maximum-Entropy Conjecture for Black-Hole Mergers with GW250114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05718)
*Yang Jiang,Qing-Guo Huang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文检验了最大熵猜想在双黑洞并合事件GW250114中的适用性，发现其预测的最终黑洞质量与自旋与环-down观测结果在误差范围内一致，支持该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数值相对论能精确描述双黑洞并合后的残余物，但决定最终状态选择的物理原理尚不明确；近期提出的最大熵猜想为此提供了一种基于贝肯斯坦-霍金熵最大化的新视角。

Method: 利用引力波事件GW250114的前后并合信号分别推断前身与残余性质，沿能量-角动量轨迹构建代理克尔熵，并将最大熵态与独立推断的最终黑洞质量及无量纲自旋进行比较。

Result: 最大熵猜想预测残余质量M_f=70.0^{+0.35}_{-0.21} M⊙、自旋χ_f=0.70^{+0.00}_{-0.01}，与环-down估计值M_f=72.3^{+3.6}_{-3.7} M⊙、χ_f=0.74^{+0.05}_{-0.07}在68%置信区间内熵偏差与零一致。

Conclusion: 该研究未发现与最大熵猜想存在显著矛盾的证据，支持其作为双黑洞并合后最终状态选择原则的合理性。

Abstract: The remnants of binary black-hole (BBH) mergers are accurately described by numerical relativity, but the physical principle governing the selection of the final states remains unclear. A recently proposed maximum-entropy conjecture suggests that a BBH merger produces the Kerr BH that maximizes the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, subject to the post-Newtonian formalism or balance laws for energy and angular momentum. In this work, we test this conjecture with the event GW250114, inferring the progenitor and remnant properties from the observed pre-merge and post-merge signals, separately. We construct the surrogate Kerr entropy associated with candidate remnant states along the inferred energy-angular-momentum trajectory. We then compare the maximum-entropy state with the independently inferred mass and dimensionless spin of the final BH. We find that the conjecture predicts a remnant mass of $M_f=70.0^{+0.35}_{-0.21}\,M_\odot$ and spin of $χ_f=0.70^{+0.00}_{-0.01}$, compared with the ringdown estimates $M_f={72.3}^{+3.6}_{-3.7}\,M_\odot$ and $χ_f=0.74^{+0.05}_{-0.07}$. The entropy deviation is consistent with zero within the $68\%$ credible interval for our fiducial analysis, and variations in the pre-merger truncation time and quasi-normal modes (QNM) content reveal no compelling discrepancy with the conjecture.

</details>


### [6] [Gravitational-Wave Microlensing as a Probe of Compact Object Population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05947)
*Eungwang Seo,Jose María Ezquiaga,Martin Hendry*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层贝叶斯框架，利用引力波微透镜效应从多个微透镜引力波事件中推断致密天体（如原初黑洞、暗物质子晕）的质量函数斜率、数密度及暗物质占比。模拟表明约100个探测事件即可有效约束关键参数，且在O4灵敏度下三年观测可实现对暗物质分数的高精度恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密天体（如原初黑洞、暗物质子晕）丰度尚不清楚，尤其因其几乎不发出电磁辐射；引力波微透镜提供了一种新探针，但需系统性统计方法从多事件中提取群体性质。

Method: 构建分层贝叶斯模型，联合分析多个微透镜引力波事件，同时推断微透镜质量函数幂律指数α_ML、数密度n₀和致密天体占暗物质比例f_DM；使用基于现实双黑洞种群的模拟微透镜信号进行验证。

Result: 约100个探测到的微透镜事件可约束α_ML与n₀（存在二者简并）；O4灵敏度下三年观测预计约130个可探测事件，能以90%置信区间[7.3×10⁻⁵, 1.8×10⁻⁴]恢复注入值f_DM≈1.2×10⁻⁴。

Conclusion: 引力波微透镜结合分层贝叶斯推断是探测非发光致密天体群体的有效统计工具，有望在未来引力波观测中精确限定原初黑洞或暗物质子晕的宇宙学丰度。

Abstract: The abundance of compact objects ranging from stellar to intermediate masses ($\sim 1 - 10^{5}M_{\odot}$) remains poorly constrained, particularly for populations such as primordial black holes and dark matter subhalos that emit little or no electromagnetic radiation. Gravitational-wave (GW) microlensing provides a complementary way to probe these populations through wave-optics effects imprinted on the GW signals, with implications for astrophysical and cosmological inference. We develop a hierarchical Bayesian framework to infer the population properties of microlenses from an ensemble of microlensed GW events, constraining the slope of the power-law lens mass function ($α_{\rm ML}$), the lens number density ($n_0$), and the fraction of dark matter in compact lenses ($f_{\rm DM}$). Using simulated microlensed GW signals drawn from a realistic binary black hole population, we find that $\sim 100$ detected microlensed events are able to constrain both $α_{\rm ML}$ and $n_0$, although a degeneracy between the two remains. For a low-mass-dominated lens population ($α_{\rm ML}=2$) with $n_0=10^6~\mathrm{Mpc}^{-3}$, a three-year observation at the O4 sensitivity yields $\sim 130$ detectable microlensed events and recovers the injected dark-matter fraction, $f_{\rm DM}\simeq 1.2 \times 10^{-4}$, with a 90% credible interval of $7.3 \times 10^{-5} \lesssim f_{\rm DM} \lesssim 1.8 \times 10^{-4}$.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文评估了Euclid DR1数据发布前，巡天掩模和非均匀天区覆盖对星系团两点相关函数及其协方差的影响；结果表明，在合理范围内，这些系统效应不会显著偏置聚类振幅，但会增加协方差，可通过协方差模型中的 nuisance 参数校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对Stage-IV巡天（如Euclid）产生的大尺度星系团样本对宇宙学测量精度提出的更高要求，需严格控制观测系统误差，特别是掩模和天区覆盖不均匀性对星系团聚类统计的影响。

Method: 基于DR1风格的光锥模拟（lightcone mocks），系统测试了不规则巡天掩模及空间非均匀性（即不同天区星系团选择函数的变化）对两点相关函数及其协方差的影响，并分析偏差与选择函数最大变化幅度、区域数量之间的关系。

Result: 不规则掩模在随机样本使用相同掩模时不影响两点相关函数；选择函数的空间变化在DR1预期水平内不显著偏置聚类振幅；当变化足够大时，偏差主要取决于选择函数的最大跨区差异而非区域数目；掩模几何与天区不均匀性均使协方差增大，表现为近似尺度无关的小偏移。

Conclusion: DR1中掩模相关系统误差可控，现有处理方法稳健；结果为Euclid后续星系团宇宙学分析提供了方法论支撑和协方差建模改进方向。

Abstract: Wide-area cluster catalogues from Stage-IV surveys will substantially improve the precision of cluster clustering measurements, making the control of observational systematic effects essential for cosmological inference. In preparation for the first Euclid data release (DR1), we assess the impact of the survey mask and non-homogeneous sky coverage on the two-point correlation function of galaxy clusters and its covariance. Using DR1-like lightcone mocks, we confirm that the irregular footprint does not introduce appreciable changes in the two-point correlation function when the same mask is applied to the random catalogue. We also find that spatial inhomogeneities, implemented as variations of the cluster selection function in different patches of the sky, do not significantly bias the clustering amplitude, up to levels of variation well above those expected for DR1. When the degree of inhomogeneity is increased to the regime where the effect becomes significant, we show that its amplitude is related to the maximum variation of the selection function among different regions, rather than to the number of such regions. We further find that irregular mask geometry and non-uniform sky coverage both increase the clustering covariance, introducing small, nearly scale-independent offsets that can be captured by nuisance parameters in the covariance model. Our results consolidate the treatment of mask systematic errors for DR1 and inform the methodological refinements required for future \Euclid cluster cosmology.

</details>


### [8] [Primordial Black Holes and Induced Gravitational Waves from Inflation with History-Dependent Effective Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06329)
*Narges Rashidi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种带有指数因子修正的暴胀模型，该因子依赖于标量场和累积膨胀历史；通过变分法导出含终值边界条件的伴随响应，发现慢滚参数ε₁可出现瞬态正极小，从而在标量功率谱上产生局域峰值，进而生成可观测的原初黑洞及对应的引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能产生显著原初黑洞（PBH）和可探测标量诱导引力波（SIGW）的暴胀机制，特别是通过引入与膨胀历史耦合的指数修正项来调控小尺度功率谱增强。

Because a non-zero Ricci curvature grows and by analogy with black hole scalarisation, the latter black hole formation mechanism is dubbed \emph{curvaturisation}. We explicitly provide that effective metric in terms of the background and the Lagrangian functions, and show that it shares the same causality structure as the background metric of curvaturised black holes. By turning to the Schrödinger-like formulation of the perturbations, we analyse the scattering potential and conclude that the axial perturbations of curvaturised black holes are linearly stable. Using Bayesian analyses of the binary-neutron-star events GW170817 and GW190425, we obtain a combined constraint on the quadratic-curvature coupling of $a\lesssim6.2\times10^6\,\mathrm{m}^2$ at $90\%$ credibility, corresponding to a lower bound on the scalar mass of $m_s\gtrsim3.2\times10^{-11}\,\mathrm{eV}$. Its trace is the mean parton number, and normalization gives a sampled parton's entropy. A compatible thermal state, if it exists, maximizes entropy at fixed one-parton matrix. This maximum equals the actual entropy only for that thermal state and measures entanglement only for a specified subsystem of a pure state. The geometric multiplicity entropy follows if all emitted partons share one mode. An entropy computed from densities becomes such a maximum, an upper bound on the actual entropy, only for specified resolved modes. We also show that the state averaged over unrecorded emissions, assumed incoherent across scales, obeys probability-preserving Lindblad evolution on finitely many regulated modes with the resolution scale if emission and no-emission terms match. The occupations reproduce leading-order DGLAP, with virtual terms, at leading logarithmic accuracy as the cutoffs on momentum fractions are removed. The reduction holds for strongly ordered transverse momenta in light-cone gauge and dilute occupations, including those of newly resolved modes. Approximating momentum and helicity modes by independent thermal populations closes the occupation equation and gives this bound's evolution, with Bose and Pauli factors from the operator algebra. The test compares the mode number inferred from normalized hadron factorial moments in a rapidity window with the window's transverse mode count.

For real, flavour-aligned couplings, the local response is specified by four combinations of coupling shifts. Abstract: We present a theoretically constrained phenomenological reconstruction of the unpolarized, chiral-even, leading-twist proton generalized parton distributions $H^q$ and $E^q$. The construction imposes exact valence and anomalous-magnetic-moment normalizations, the low-scale condition $B^{u+d}(0)=0$, the required endpoint behavior, and the complete Gaussian impact-parameter positivity bound. We examine electromagnetic and energy--momentum-tensor form factors, impact-parameter distributions, positivity, polynomiality, and illustrative mechanical properties. Abstract: Revisiting a duality model in which dual QCD becomes hydrodynamics, or the dual gluon is a fluid, allows us to understand some of the mysteries of QCD, such as the behavior of the quark--gluon plasma (QGP) as a perfect fluid and gluon saturation in the structure function at high energies. This old-fashioned duality converts the gluon action into hydrodynamics described by the 2-form stream function $W^a_{μν}$ with eight colors. All interactions then become current--current interactions of the Nambu--Jona-Lasinio type. The resulting theory admits a strong-coupling expansion about the fluid. This can provide a third method to supplement perturbative QCD and lattice QCD.

This resulted in the cavity resonators of the MIT bag model, in the vibrating strings inherited from the dual resonance model, in the Budapest membrane bag, and in a constituent-gluon potential model. The idea was subsequently developed in the early 1980s into a phenomenology capable of predicting masses, quantum numbers, and decay signatures. This article traces that history from the primary literature, giving close attention to dates of submission and to who first proposed what, and supplements the written record with recollections of many of the principal participants. Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of accelerator-based neutrino oscillations to localized electron-density enhancements associated with seismic fault zones along the Fermilab SURF baseline of the Deep Underground Neutrino Experiment (DUNE). Consequently, detecting sufficiently persistent density anomalies may be possible under favourable conditions, whereas short-term earthquake forecasting is not feasible with the nominal DUNE configuration. Abstract: Negative weights are a major cost in NLO event generation matched to parton showers at the LHC, as they increase the number of events needed for a given statistical precision. Abstract: We extend our work to compute the ground-state baryon mass spectrum containing charm and bottom quarks in the relativistic independent quark (RIQ) model with an average flavor--independent confining potential in equally mixed scalar--vector harmonic form. Abstract: Since the dawn of the Space Age, the Moon has been considered one of the most promising locations for astronomical observations. The absence of atmosphere, exceptional mechanical stability, access to the permanently shielded radio-quiet far side, and the possibility of constructing extremely large apertures have motivated numerous concepts for lunar observatories over the past six decades. Despite extensive studies conducted during the Apollo era, most proposed lunar astronomy projects remained unrealized due to technological limitations, shifting scientific priorities, and the high cost of sustained lunar operations. Today, renewed international interest in lunar exploration, combined with advances in robotics, autonomous systems, additive manufacturing, precision optics, and formation flying, is reopening the prospect of large-scale astronomical infrastructures on the Moon. In particular, lunar hypertelescope concepts offer the possibility of achieving unprecedented angular resolutions through diluted optical arrays spanning hundreds of meters to several kilometres. This paper reviews the historical evolution of lunar astronomy concepts, analyses the reasons behind previous failures, examines current technological developments, and proposes a roadmap toward future lunar hypertelescope facilities capable of transforming observational astronomy during the twenty-first century.

Abstract: Near-Earth Asteroids (NEAs) represent both a scientific opportunity and a potential threat to human civilization. While current survey programs have successfully detected most large NEAs, a significant observational gap remains for objects smaller than approximately 140 m, which can still produce regional-scale devastation. Ground-based surveys are intrinsically limited by atmospheric conditions, weather, and solar avoidance constraints, which leave a significant fraction of near-Sun objects difficult to detect. In particular, Earth-based observatories remain largely blind to objects approaching from the Sun-ward direction, reducing available warning times. Future lunar observatories offer a promising solution to this limitation. A telescope operating on the Moon could enable observations of regions inaccessible from Earth, provide enhanced sensitivity, and significantly improve early detection capabilities. However, the lunar environment imposes severe engineering constraints, including thermal cycling, dust contamination, radiation exposure, and autonomous operation requirements. We propose the AMAS(Atacama-Mongolia-Antarctica-Sahara) project, an international initiative using Earth's most extreme environments as analogue sites for future lunar astronomical facilities. By deploying and testing specialized NEA survey telescopes in these environments, key technologies for future lunar observatories can be validated under realistic operational conditions. This paper presents the scientific rationale, engineering challenges, site selection strategy, and long-term roadmap toward a global Earth-Moon network for planetary defense.

Abstract: Gravitational arcs produced by strong gravitational lensing provide a unique approach for investigating dark matter distribution, galaxy cluster mass reconstruction and the structure of the Universe. Abstract: Context. Deep photometric surveys increasingly rely on low signal-to-noise imaging, where noise affects source detection, flux measurements, photometric redshifts, and sample selection. Self-supervised denoising methods are attractive because they do not require ground-truth images, but applying them to quantitative photometry requires controlling the flux biases they may introduce. Aims. We evaluate and improve Noise2Void (N2V) for denoising real astronomical images, focusing on preserving fluxes, reducing photometric scatter, and retaining the main source features across a wide magnitude range. Methods. We use real observations from the Physics of the Accelerating Universe Survey (PAUS) together with PAUS-like simulated images. We show that standard N2V systematically underestimates galaxy fluxes, especially for faint galaxies. To mitigate this bias, we add a bounded-output layer that limits large negative outputs, and fine-tune the model with low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and two flux-aware loss terms that preserve the flux scale and improve repeated-exposure flux consistency. Results. For PAUS galaxies with 22 <= i_AB < 23 in the NB645 band, the standard N2V flux-ratio distribution peaks far below unity, while the bounded-output model restores it close to the original PAUS flux scale. Conclusions. Denoising methods for astronomical surveys must be evaluated with photometric diagnostics, not image-quality metrics alone. With flux-aware modifications, self-supervised denoising can be adapted to low-SNR survey images while preserving the flux information needed for quantitative analysis.

### [86] [Fast and accurate false-alarm probabilities for linear detection statistics in Poisson noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05510)
*Maayane T. Soumagnac,Eran O. Ofek,Shachar Israeli,David Glukhov*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于FFT和Panjer递推的高效精确方法，用于计算泊松噪声下任意线性检测统计量的虚警概率，显著优于传统模拟方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光子匮乏的天文图像等低计数测量中，泊松噪声下的统计显著性（虚警概率）计算困难，现有方法依赖大量仿真。

Method: 将检测统计量建模为复合泊松随机变量，利用快速傅里叶变换（FFT）和Panjer递推实现高效精确计算。

Result: 该方法在数值离散精度内精确，比仿真快多个数量级，可计算仿真无法企及的虚警概率，并提供开源Python和MATLAB实现。

Conclusion: 该框架将泊松噪声下的检测显著性计算从仿真问题转变为确定性数值计算，不仅适用于天文成像，也广泛适用于其他泊松事件检测问题。

Abstract: Detecting weak signals in noisy data often requires calculating the statistical significance, or false- alarm probability, of a detection statistic. When the underlying noise follows Poisson statistics, as in low-count measurements such as photon-starved astronomical images, this calculation becomes non- trivial, and existing approaches typically rely on extensive simulations to estimate detection thresholds. We derive an accurate and efficient solution for computing false-alarm probabilities for arbitrary linear detection statistics under Poisson noise. We show that the detection statistic is a compound Poisson random variable, which unlocks efficient implementations based on the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) and the Panjer recursion. The method is exact up to numerical discretization, accelerates computation by several orders of magnitude compared with simulations, and enables evaluation of false-alarm probabilities computationally inaccessible through simulation. Although motivated by low-count astronomical imaging, the framework applies broadly to detection problems involving Poisson-distributed events. It transforms the computation of detection significance under Poisson noise from a simulation problem into a deterministic numerical calculation. We provide open-source Python and MATLAB implementations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过分析光学与近红外波段偏振光子所携带的信息，结合谱偏振测量对光子统计量的需求（每谱元超百万光子），论证大型望远镜特别是GSMTs的必要性。

Result: 指出GSMTs建成后将提供前所未有的集光能力，使高精度天文偏振测量成为可能，从而开启新发现时代。

Conclusion: GSMTs是推动高精度天文偏振测量发展的关键基础设施，将极大拓展我们对天体物理过程的理解能力。

Abstract: The photons we collect from astronomical sources are encoded with information about the physics behind their origin. To fully probe that physics, we must maximize the information we extract from those photons. At optical and near-infrared wavelengths, this is typically done with the use of filters or a disperser to extract flux as a function of color. An often disregarded piece of information that is also carried by those photons is the polarization state. Astronomical polarimetry is capable of revealing details about the source geometry and magnetic fields that are often unresolvable and, therefore, inaccessible by any other means. Polarimetry is typically a photon-starved science because crucial model discriminators are often only revealed when precisions of 0.1% in the degree of polarization are achieved. For spectropolarimetry, this necessitates the collection of over one million photons at each spectral bin, which demands the use of large aperture telescopes for typical objects of interest. The future completion of Giant Segmented Mirror Telescopes (GSMTs) promises the long-awaited light grasp to initiate an unprecedented era of discovery with astronomical polarimetry.

</details>

Conclusion: 亚角秒低频射电成像已具备大视场、高深度、高分辨率同步实现的可行性，标志着低频射电天文进入新阶段，为LOFAR2.0与SKA时代的大规模巡天提供了关键技术验证与方法论基础。

Abstract: We present ultra-deep sub-arcsecond radio images of the ELAIS-N1 field covering 2.5x2.5 deg^2, produced from 200 hours of LOw Frequency ARray (LOFAR) observations in the 115-168 MHz band. The radio continuum images are obtained using recent advances in LOFAR long-baseline calibration and imaging. We achieve a central point-source sensitivity of 5.3 $μ$Jy/beam at an angular resolution of 0.33"x0.39" and an area-weighted sensitivity of 7.4 $μ$Jy/beam at 0.36"x0.45", making these the deepest radio images below 1.28 GHz to date and among the deepest at any radio frequency. By combining our data with previously released deep 6" LOFAR images, we compile a catalogue with a total of 62,916 unique radio sources across 2.5x2.5 deg^2. Our calibration strategy, combined with astrometric alignment using 95 Gaia quasars, results in positional offsets of $Δ$RA = -3 $\pm$ 27 mas and $Δ$DEC = 2 $\pm$ 17 mas for these same sources. We further demonstrate the potential for deep polarisation studies at sub-arcsecond resolution with instrumental leakages from Stokes I into linear polarisation down to 0.12%, which is about a factor of two better than the typical leakage in the LOFAR Two-metre Sky Survey at 6" resolution. Our results demonstrate that imaging a large number of observations over a wide field area at sub-arcsecond resolution is now computationally feasible. This establishes a new benchmark for low-frequency radio imaging in terms of combining depth, resolution, and survey area, which lays the foundation for deeper and wider surveys in the LOFAR2.0 and SKA era.

### [89] [Developing the Field Selector System for the SHARP Multi-IFU: Optomechanical Design and First Prototype Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06332)

Conclusion: FSS是一种新颖且实用的多IFU视场选择方案，其光机同步设计理念有效解决了多探针共焦成像的关键难题，具备良好的工程落地潜力和推广价值。

Abstract: This paper presents the innovative Field Selector System (FSS) developed for VESPER, the multi-Integral Field Unit of the SHARP near-infrared spectrograph. The proposed system employs an array of movable light-collection probes (Field Selectors) that travel along parallel, vertically offset planes to avoid mechanical interference and vignetting. The true novelty lies in its opto-mechanical synchronization: as each probe translates by Δy to select a target, a set of folding mirrors shifts by Δy/2. This maintains a constant optical path length, allowing multiple independently moving probes to share a single focal plane and form a continuous image on it, easy to slice by the slicer. A dedicated optical test setup was developed to characterize the positioning stability and repeatability of the probe motion. The measured mechanical errors were converted into equivalent optical tilts and incorporated into the SHARP optical model to assess their impact on instrument performance. The experimental results identified the dominant sources of positioning error and provided valuable insight into the relationship between mechanical tolerances and optical performance. The study also highlights several design improvements capable of significantly reducing probe tilting and enhancing system stability. The results demonstrate the viability of the proposed concept and indicate that, with the identified improvements, the system represents an excellent field-selection solution for SHARP and other multi-integral-field-unit (mIFU) spectrographs.

</details>

Abstract: The Extremely Large Telescopes (ELTs), with their large apertures and cutting-edge Multi-Conjugate Adaptive Optics (MCAO) systems, promise to deliver data sharper and deeper than even the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) across large fields. SHARP is a concept study for a near-IR (0.95--2.45 $μ$m) spectrograph specifically designed to fully exploit the collecting area and angular resolution capabilities of the upcoming ESO's ELT. The instrument concept is driven by the goal of tackling the most important questions in astrophysics and cosmology, bridging the gap between the local and distant Universe. This requires versatility to accommodate diverse observational needs. SHARP (http://sharp.brera.inaf.it) is composed of two main units: NEXUS, a Multi-Object Spectrograph (MOS) optimized for detecting the faintest sources, and VESPER, a multi-object Integral Field Unit (multi-IFU) designed for brighter ones. This presentation provides an overview of the scientific design drivers, the solutions developed to meet them, and the resulting optical design that achieves the required performance.

### [91] [Object-independent evaluation of small-data astronomical image classifiers: architecture, representation, and acquisition shortcuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06484)
*Eraldo Pereira Marinho*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个名为\dataset{}的小型天文图像数据集，用于评估分类器在天体形态识别任务中的泛化能力，并揭示图像上下文、观测设置和星表属性对分类性能的影响。实验表明，高准确率并不必然反映模型学习到了目标天体的形态特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小样本天文图像数据集的报告性能可能受到目标重用、视场差异、观测设置及星表派生属性等因素干扰，而非真正反映模型对天体形态的学习能力，因此需要一个能分离这些混杂因素的基准数据集。

Method: 构建了包含370个真实物理天体（四类：星系、球状星团、星云、疏散星团）的\dataset{}数据集，每个天体由三波段DSS FITS图像表示，并采用按天体分组的训练/验证/测试划分；对比了自研CNN-Transformer、ImageNet预训练ResNet-50与ViT-B/16在多种图像表示方式（自适应全视场、统一分辨率视场、中心掩膜控制）下的性能，并使用三次固定随机种子和集群自助法评估稳定性。

Result: 在物体独立的一次性测试中，自研模型、ResNet-50和ViT-B/16在自适应视场下的平均平衡准确率分别为0.773±0.014、0.874±0.054和0.866±0.023；经Holm校正后，主架构间无统计显著差异；控制实验显示，仅靠观测与星表元数据即可达0.721–0.735准确率，中心掩膜视图达0.731–0.800，证实上下文与非形态信息贡献显著。

Conclusion: 模型在\dataset{}上展现出对未见物理天体的良好泛化能力，但高分类准确率主要源于图像上下文、观测条件及星表属性等非形态线索，而非目标天体的真实形态特征；因此，在此类小天文数据集上，不能将高精度简单归因于形态学习，且架构比较无法脱离预训练影响而孤立评估。

Abstract: Reported performance in small astronomical image datasets can reflect object reuse, field-of-view differences, acquisition settings, and catalogue-derived properties as well as astronomical morphology. We designed \dataset{} to measure these effects in a four-class task comprising galaxies, globular clusters, nebulae, and open clusters. The dataset contains 370 catalogue-supported physical objects, each represented by three Digitised Sky Survey FITS bands, and uses a group-constrained split of 222 training, 75 validation, and 73 test objects. We compared a custom CNN--Transformer trained from scratch with ImageNet-pretrained ResNet-50 and ViT-B/16 under an adaptive full-field representation, a common angular field, and two central-region-masked controls, using three fixed seeds. In the one-pass object-independent test, mean balanced accuracy on the adaptive view was $0.773\pm0.014$, $0.874\pm0.054$, and $0.866\pm0.023$ for the custom model, ResNet-50, and ViT-B/16, respectively. No primary architecture contrast rejected its null after Holm correction; ResNet-50 and ViT-B/16 differed by 0.007 balanced-accuracy points (95\% cluster-bootstrap interval $[-0.048,0.062]$). Controls based on acquisition and catalogue metadata reached 0.721--0.735, and central-region-masked adaptive views reached 0.731--0.800. These results demonstrate generalisation to unseen physical objects, but also show that image context, acquisition settings, and catalogue-derived object properties carry substantial class information. In this regime, high accuracy cannot be interpreted as evidence that a classifier learned target morphology, and the comparison does not isolate architecture from pretraining.

</details>

Method: 通过分析MORFEO系统的光学设计、大气层析重建、激光与自然导星协同传感、焦平面稳定性监测及遥测数据驱动的PSF建模方法，评估其综合MCAO性能。

Result: MORFEO可提供~1′视场内稳定的衍射极限PSF；利用6颗钠激光导星+最多3颗自然导星实现约50%的南银极天区覆盖；满足MICADO和HARMONI的输入要求，并为SHARP提供基准MCAO性能参考。

Abstract: MORFEO is the multi-conjugate adaptive optics (MCAO) relay for the Extremely Large Telescope (ELT) of the European Southern Observatory. It combines the adaptive mirrors of the telescope (M4-M5) with two post-focal deformable mirrors to deliver a stable, diffraction-limited point spread function over a field approaching 1'. MORFEO senses the atmosphere using six sodium laser guide stars and up to three natural guide stars distributed across a $\sim$160'' technical field, enabling sky coverage levels of order 50% at the South Galactic Pole. Through its two gravity-invariant ports, MORFEO feeds the MICADO first-light imager and spectrograph with a corrected field of 51''and HARMONI, the high-angular resolution integral field spectrograph. SHARP, a concept for a multi-object near-infrared spectrograph at the ELT, adopts MORFEO as its reference MCAO architecture for evaluating achievable image quality and scientific performance. In this paper, we summarise the MCAO capabilities delivered by MORFEO, its optical quality, tomographic performance, stability of the corrected focal plane, and the accuracy of telemetry-based PSF reconstruction, and discuss their relevance for the development of SHARP and other wide-field spectroscopic concepts at the ELT.

Result: 在HR 4796A可见光波段的实测MagAO-X图像上验证了该方法，显著提升了原行星盘结构的信噪比与细节恢复能力，证明了WDH可建模性与WFS遥测用于噪声校正的有效性。

Conclusion: WFS遥测数据可用于精确建模动态WDH噪声，所提出的windsoCC+参数化重建流程是一种高效、物理驱动的WDH校正新范式，有望推广至其他高对比度成像系统。

Abstract: The wind-driven halo (WDH) is a persistent, low spatial frequency noise artifact that arises due to the servo-lag error inherent to all adaptive optics (AO) instruments. Spatial filtering may be employed to overcome this artifact, however, filtering out the WDH while simultaneously preserving signal from an extended astrophysical object of interest is exceptionally challenging. Additionally, since the WDH changes in intensity and position angle through an observation, data-driven algorithms (e.g., KLIP) that are commonly used to subtract the starlight need to be overly-aggressive to remove both the static and dynamic noise components. Since wavefront sensors (WFSs) continuously track the closed-loop residual wavefront error, WFS telemetry presents the ideal resource for combating this type of noise artifact through postprocessing. Using archival WFS telemetry from MagAO-X, which is the "extreme" AO instrument for the 6.5-meter Magellan-Clay telescope, we demonstrate a novel workflow for WDH reconstruction and removal in individual coronagraphic science images. MagAO-X is equipped with a pyramid WFS capable of recording wavefront telemetry at a high-cadence which is saved during data acquisition. Given this, we detail how our WFS data processing pipeline, windsoCC, cross-correlates the recorded closed-loop wavefront to measure the wind vectors of several turbulent layers of the atmosphere above Las Campanas Observatory. We then make use of the wind parameters learned through windsoCC to reconstruct the WDH footprint by leveraging a parametric model. Notably, we demonstrate a dramatic improvement in object recovery using on-sky MagAO-X images of the disk around HR 4796A at visible wavelengths.

</details>

Abstract: One of the most promising applications of quantum computing is the ability to simulate quantum systems. A quantity of particular importance in condensed matter physics is the ground-state energy of a system. Here, we present an implementation of a hybrid quantum-classical algorithm known as the variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) to estimate the ground state energy of a prototypical quantum spin system. For this spin system, we use the transverse field Ising model (TFIM) which is known to exhibit a quantum phase transition (QPT) at a critical point. We apply the VQE to a $4$-spin TFIM and evaluate its performance using both a local noise model and IBM quantum hardware to estimate the ground-state energy and reproduce the QPT. The resulting ground-state energy estimates and QPT behaviour are compared with exact-diagonalisation benchmarks.

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Jacobs于1983年提出的'手性无序费米子'(CDF)格点狄拉克算符，证明其无序平均后严格满足手征对称性{D_eff,γ5}=0，但该对称性导致轴向反常无法再现，因其轴向指数在所有定义下（包括例外点）均为零；文章进一步按无序所处的狄拉克通道（γμ或单位矩阵通道）对手征无序格点费米子进行分类，统一了格点QCD去双重化与无序狄拉克费米子的十重分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决手性无序费米子（CDF）能否作为满足手征对称性的格点费米子方案的问题，厘清其与轴向反常、指标定理及拓扑分类的关系。

Method: 运用现代非厄米算符理论、无序平均方法、指标定义（Hermitian迹、双正交指标、Riesz投影指标）以及Altland-Zirnbauer对称性分类框架，分析CDF算符的代数结构与谱性质。

Result: 证明CDF算符无序平均后严格满足手征对称性但轴向指数恒为零，无法再现轴向反常；发现无序所在狄拉克通道决定物理性质：γμ通道保持手征性但无拓扑指标，单位矩阵通道破坏手征性并呈现domain-wall物理；构造一参数插值族，连接1983年CDF与Kaplan domain-wall模型。

Conclusion: CDF虽严格保手征对称，却因非厄米性与零指标而不能复现轴向反常；格点手征费米子的实现不取决于是否引入无序，而取决于无序作用的狄拉克通道；该工作统一了格点QCD去双重化与无序费米子的十重分类框架。

Abstract: A lattice Dirac operator designed to preserve exact chiral symmetry by weighting the derivative with a quenched random field---the \emph{chiral disordered fermions} (\CDF) of [L.\ Jacobs, Phys.\ Rev.\ Lett.\ \textbf{51}, 172 (1983)]---is reexamined with modern tools. We show that the disorder-averaged operator satisfies $\anticomm{\Deff}{\gfive}=0$ exactly, to all orders in disorder strength. Disorder lifts the doubler degeneracy, driving doublers off the real axis into a complex mass while the physical mode remains protected, but does so through non-Hermiticity rather than a Ginsparg--Wilson relation. Consequently the axial index vanishes in every formulation: the Hermitian trace $\Tr(\gfive D)$, the bi-orthogonal index for non-Hermitian operators, and the algebraic Riesz-projector index, including at exceptional points. The \CDF operator therefore cannot reproduce the axial anomaly: its failure is enforced by the exact chiral symmetry it preserves. We classify neighboring constructions by the Dirac channel carrying the lattice structure. Disorder in the $\g_μ$ channel preserves chirality, realizes an Altland--Zirnbauer chiral class, and cannot carry an index; disorder in the $\mathbb{1}$ mass channel breaks chiral symmetry and yields Callan--Harvey/domain-wall physics. A one-parameter family interpolates between them, with the 1983 operator and Kaplan's domain wall as opposite limits, distinguished not by disorder itself but by the channel it occupies. This unifies lattice-QCD undoubling with the tenfold-way classification of disordered Dirac fermions and settles the disorder route to chiral lattice fermions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用带交错费米子的欧几里得格点公式，并进行对偶变换，将原自由度映射为plaquette、dimer和massive情形下的monomer构型；设计并实现适配该对偶表示的局部与非局部蒙特卡洛更新算法，并对质量项引入的情形分析重加权法处理符号问题的效果。

Result: 计算了平均plaquette、dimer密度、手征凝聚、平均符号等可观测量，刻画了其对质量、耦合常数和θ的依赖关系，并识别出符号问题最显著的参数区域。

Abstract: This thesis studies the Schwinger model in (1 + 1) dimensions using a lattice formulation and its dual representation. The model provides a simple yet non-trivial setting for investigating characteristic phenomena of gauge theories, including screening, confinement-related behaviour, the Schwinger effect and dependence on the topological angle ($θ$). After introducing the Euclidean formulation with staggered fermions, we describe the dual transformation in which the original degrees of freedom are replaced by configurations of plaquettes, dimers and, in the massive case, monomers.
  The main objective is to investigate the numerical behaviour of the model in the presence of a fermion mass and a topological angle. We implement Monte Carlo algorithms adapted to the dual representation, including local and non-local updates that allow admissible configurations to be sampled. For the massive model, we also analyse the emergence of the sign problem and its effect on the estimation of observables through reweighting.
  From the simulations we compute several quantities, including the average plaquette, the dimer density, the chiral condensate and the average sign. These observables characterise the dependence on the mass, coupling and topological angle ($θ$), and identify the regions in which the sign problem becomes most significant. Finally, we discuss the numerical results and the usefulness of the dual formulation for investigating gauge theories in non-perturbative regimes.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限体积分析中壳上减法的修正项随质量参数减小的变化规律，发现非零质量下修正项呈指数压制，而无质量极限下则出现增强的有限体积依赖性及S波红外发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限体积分析中，壳上减法用于分离壳上奇点并评估动量相关因子的壳上值；但其修正项在质量参数趋近于零时的行为尚不明确，尤其对长程作用（如QCD+QED）影响显著。

Conclusion: 明确了壳上减法的适用条件，为含长程效应（如QCD+QED）的强子有限体积计算中评估此类修正提供了理论依据和实用指导。

Abstract: On-shell subtraction is a relevant step in finite-volume analyses, which is to isolate an on-shell singularity and evaluate regular momentum-dependent factors at their on-shell values. For short-range interactions with a finite mass gap, the correction associated with this on-shell subtraction is exponentially suppressed at sufficiently large volume and can therefore be neglected at the corresponding accuracy. We examine how this correction changes as the relevant mass parameter decreases. Using a common dimensional-regularization prescription, we derive analytic expressions for the resulting finite volume correction for representative momentum structures and examine their behavior numerically. For a nonzero mass parameter, the corrections are indeed exponentially suppressed at large $μL$, whereas in the massless limit this suppression is replaced by an enhanced finite-volume dependence. More importantly, the $S$-wave contribution can develop an infrared divergence of finite volume correction in the massless limit. These results clarify the conditions under which on-shell subtraction can be applied and provide guidance for assessing such corrections in finite-volume hadronic calculations involving long-range effects, including QCD+QED.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We construct a quantum measurement protocol for estimating the Rényi entanglement asymmetry, which quantifies symmetry breaking at the level of a subsystem and provides a probe of the quantum Mpemba effect. The protocol combines the SWAP test with a charge-resolving procedure based on randomized symmetry rotations. For a fixed additive accuracy $ε$, the required number of measurements scales as $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-2})$ without explicit dependence on the total system size, making the protocol particularly attractive for quantum many-body systems and lattice quantum field theories. As a representative application, we study the quantum Mpemba effect in the lattice Schwinger model with a $θ$ term using classical state-vector simulations. We further estimate the resources required to implement the protocol on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices, fault-tolerant quantum computers (FTQCs), and a representative early-FTQC architecture. Our results provide a measurement-efficient framework for investigating anomalous symmetry-restoration dynamics in quantum many-body systems and quantum field theories.

*Laurens Lootens,Hersh Singh*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性规范理论是标准模型的基础，但至今尚无非微扰模拟方法；现有格点构造面临Nielsen-Ninomiya定理障碍，导致费米子测度复杂或局域希尔伯特空间无限维。

Abstract: Chiral gauge theories underlie the Standard Model, yet none has been simulated nonperturbatively. Lattice constructions that evade the Nielsen-Ninomiya obstruction entail complex fermion measures or infinite-dimensional local Hilbert spaces. Here we provide a gauge-invariant Hamiltonian lattice regularization of 1+1-dimensional abelian chiral gauge theories built from clock-and-shift qudits with $\mathbb{Z}_N$ gauge fields, in which the global chiral symmetry and its 't Hooft anomaly survive exactly mod $N$. Anomaly cancellation on the lattice lets the Gauss laws be imposed and solved exactly, reducing the theory to an unconstrained qudit chain. Using infinite matrix product states, we simulate the canonical 1+1-dimensional chiral gauge theory, the 34-50 model, and find excellent agreement with analytic predictions, showing that a finite-dimensional local Hilbert space at $N=6$ reproduces the expected infrared physics of the continuum 34-50 model. This provides an efficient and practical solution to the problem of simulating general abelian chiral gauge theories in 1+1 dimensions, both on classical and quantum computers.

### [100] [How sky subtraction scales affect Intracluster light measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05467)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深空成像巡天揭示低表面亮度宇宙，但天空背景估计若在与天体尺度相当的范围内进行，会引入流量测量偏差，尤其影响星系团内光（ICL）这一最延展的弥散重子成分。

Conclusion: 天空背景扣除尺度是影响星系团内光测量准确性的关键系统误差源，需依据目标天体尺度谨慎选择；未来LSST全量数据处理策略有望改善该问题。

Abstract: Deep imaging surveys are opening a new window on the low surface brightness universe. However, the recovery of diffuse emission depends on the survey depth and how the sky background is treated. Estimating the sky on scales comparable to astrophysical sources risks biasing flux measurements. This effect is particularly significant for intracluster light, the most extended diffuse baryonic component of galaxy clusters. We show in a $M_{500}$--$z$ plane that the sky subtraction scales employed in the Legacy Survey of Space and Time Data Preview 2 and Hyper Suprime-Cam Public Data Release 3 (HSC-PDR3) impact clusters with $M_{500}\gtrsim10^{13.4}\,\mathrm{M_\odot}$ at all redshifts, whereas HSC-PDR2 only oversubtracts the most massive systems at $z < 0.1$. The larger sky subtraction scale anticipated for the full LSST survey will mitigate this bias.

</details>


### [101] [CosmoVerse Data Challenge: How Tip of the Red Giant Branch Analysis Choices Shape the Inferred Hubble Constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05468)
*Siyang Li,Eleonora Di Valentino,Jackson Levi Said,Kayla Owens,Yukei Murakami,Louise Breuval,Teresa Sicignano,Luis A. Escamilla,Jéferson A. S. Fortunato,Payam Ghafari,Ayush Hazarika,Vasiliki Karanasou,Trisha Khan,Yoelsy Leyva,Mahdi Najafi,Swayamtrupta Panda,Masroor C. Pookkillath,Antonio Quintana-Estellés,Masoume Reyhani,Nandan Roy,Chandra Shekhar Saraf,Rahul Shah,Jess Worsley,Alejandro Aguirre,Miguel Barroso Varela,Anupam Bhardwaj,Elliot Dutnall,Francesca Di Nella,Hussain Gohar,Paridhi Jain,Gracelynn Jost,Shivani Kulkarni,Dhiraj Kuniyal,Mariia Marinichenko,Yash Mehta,Vasudev Mittal,Lily Myers,Devaraja Mallesha Naik,José Antonio Nájera,Pilar Ruiz-Lapuente,Kathan Dave,Elaina Sadler,Gurnoor Sandhu,Sarang Shah,Petar Suman,Stefano Casertano,Alexei V. Filippenko,Richard Stiskalek,Syed Ashraf Uddin,Duško Borka,Vesna Borka Jovanović,Youri Carloni,Umut Demirbozan,Predrag Jovanović,Mohammad Malekjani,Nils A. Nilsson,Saeed Pourojaghi,Jorge Venzor,Lilia Anguelova,Marika Asgari,Nihan Katırcı,María Pérez Garrote,Leandros Perivolaropoulos,Pradyumn Kumar Sahoo,Denitsa Staicova*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究通过CosmoVerse数据挑战，评估了基于红巨星支尖端（TRGB）方法测定哈勃常数H₀时，不同光度测量选择、样本选取和分析设置对结果的影响。26份独立提交结果显示出较大离散（中位数77.10 km/s/Mpc，标准差7.38），远超单次分析的典型误差，揭示了当前TRGB距离阶梯中系统性选择依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRGB法测定H₀的结果依赖于光度数据选取、恒星测光分析及校准星系样本构建等主观选择，亟需量化这些选择对最终H₀值的影响程度。

Method: 组织开放的数据挑战：参与者在统一的距离阶梯框架（共用Ia型超新星样本与拟合流程）下，独立选择测光星表、目标星系及TRGB测量参数；使用公共notebook和培训支持，确保方法透明可复现。

Result: 26份提交结果H₀中位数为77.10 km/s/Mpc，标准差7.38 km/s/Mpc（约是单次误差的3.4倍）；NGC 4258等锚定星系TRGB测量差异导致整体距离标度系统性偏移。

Conclusion: 相同分析框架下不同选择可导致显著不同的H₀值，凸显TRGB方法中系统性不确定性的来源；该挑战为追溯差异根源、提升数据与工具透明度提供了开放基础。

Abstract: Tip of the red giant branch (TRGB)-based determinations of the Hubble constant ($H_0$), the current expansion rate of the Universe, depend on choices made in selecting and analyzing stellar photometry and in assembling the sample of calibrator galaxies hosting Type Ia supernovae (SNe Ia). The CosmoVerse Data Challenge invites participants to analyze public photometric catalogs within a shared distance-ladder framework, opening each step of the measurement to the community and testing how these choices propagate into $H_0$. Participants independently selected photometric catalogs, galaxies, and TRGB measurement settings, while using a common SN Ia compilation and fitting procedure to convert distances into $H_0$. Supported by public notebooks and a training school, the challenge received 26 submissions from 22 participants. All submissions used the same analysis notebook, many participants were trainees at the school, and the true galaxy distances are unknown; the results therefore characterize the sensitivity of the submissions to analysis and sample-selection choices and are not a combined determination of $H_0$ or an estimate of the systematic uncertainty in published TRGB measurements. The inferred $H_0$ values have a median of $77.10\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ and a standard deviation of $7.38\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ $(5.11\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ for the scaled median absolute deviation), $\sim 3.4$ $(\sim 2.4)$ times the typical quoted uncertainty of a submission. Differences in the measured tip of the anchor galaxy NGC 4258 add a coherent shift in the distance scale. The challenge shows how different choices within a common framework can produce substantially different values of $H_0$, and provides an open basis for tracing these differences and for making datasets and tools more transparent and accessible.

</details>


### [102] [Searching for Signatures of i-process Nucleosynthesis in Metal-Rich, Lead-rich Barium Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05470)
*Catherine Manea,Madeleine McKenzie,Gail Zasowski,Giulia C. Cinquegrana,Amaya Sinha,Sarah G. Kane,Jaden Levine,Ellie Mebius,Aleksandra Winkler*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过分析六颗中等金属丰度恒星（[Fe/H] ≈ -0.7 到 -0.1）的高分辨率光谱，发现其中两颗恒星的元素丰度模式与i-过程模型高度一致，而非传统的s-或r-过程，为i-过程在近太阳金属丰度下仍可发生提供了关键观测证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证i-过程是否能在较高金属丰度（接近太阳金属丰度）下发生，因此前文献对此存疑且缺乏足够观测支持。

Method: 利用Gemini/GHOST和McDonald/Tull光谱仪获取并分析6颗GALAH巡天选出的富钡/铕恒星的高分辨率光谱；结合档案数据重新分析2颗已知i-过程候选体；测定最多42种元素丰度、^{12}C/^{13}C比及Mg同位素比，并与多组AGB模型（s-、i-过程）对比。

Result: 三颗星呈现极高[Pb/Fe]（+1.79至+2.50 dex），两颗星有极高[Eu/Fe]（+1.10至+1.30 dex）；其中两颗的丰度模式无法由s-过程解释，却与现有i-过程模型吻合；另两颗文献候选体也较好拟合i-过程模型（但s-过程未被完全排除）。

Conclusion: i-过程可在金属丰度高达[Fe/H] ≈ -0.1时发生，表明中等金属丰度AGB星能产生传统归因于r-过程的重元素；该研究确认了近太阳金属丰度下的i-过程候选体，并呼吁发展更完善的i-过程模型以理解银河系盘的化学演化。

Abstract: The intermediate neutron-capture process ($i$-process) has been invoked to explain the peculiar compositions of carbon-enhanced metal-poor (CEMP) stars enriched in both slow- ($s$) and rapid- ($r$) neutron-capture elements. Recent literature has questioned its operation at higher metallicities, but observational evidence remains limited. We analyse Gemini/GHOST and McDonald/Tull spectra of six $\rm -0.7 < [Fe/H] < -0.1$ stars identified in GALAH by their extreme Ba and Eu (or Sm) compositions ($\gtrsim$10$\times$ solar). We also reanalyse archival spectra of two literature $i$-process candidates. We determine abundances in up to 42 elements (Li through Pb), $^{12}$C/$^{13}$C, and Mg isotopic ratios, and compare to literature barium and CEMP stars and multiple asymptotic giant branch (AGB) model grids. Three stars exhibit extreme [Pb/Fe] (+1.79$\pm$0.19 to +2.50$\pm$0.32 dex), and two extreme [Eu/Fe] (+1.10$\pm$0.11 to +1.30$\pm$0.11 dex), distinguishing them from typical metal-rich barium stars. Two of these three exhibit abundance patterns that are inconsistent with $s$-process models and best explained by existing $i$-process predictions. The remaining sample is either consistent with $s$-process enrichment or suggestive of atomic diffusion. Both literature candidates are well-fit by the $i$-process model, but conventional $s$-process enrichment cannot be excluded. Our findings reinforce that metal-rich AGB stars contribute to the production of elements classically attributed to the $r$-process. These results identify promising candidates for $i$-process enrichment near solar metallicity and motivate additional $i$-process models to understand the contribution of this nucleosynthetic process to the chemical evolution of the Milky Way disk.

</details>


### [103] [Dynamics of self-gravitating halos with decaying dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05474)
*Anna Bencke,Marc Kamionkowski,Annika H. G. Peter,Juniper Morales*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了由衰变暗物质粒子构成的球形暗物质晕的演化，发现衰变产生的微小反冲速度会加热晕并使其膨胀；首次考虑了各向异性初条件的影响，并开发了模拟代码NSphere与参数-密度剖面映射的模拟器，以支持未来巡天对衰变暗物质的探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索衰变暗物质对暗物质晕结构演化的影响，特别是反冲速度加热效应及初条件各向异性带来的新物理效应，以补充现有宇宙学约束并为未来观测提供理论依据。

Method: 采用新开发的NSphere数值模拟代码，在广泛的衰变时标和反冲速度参数空间中模拟自引力球形暗物质晕的演化；首次引入各向异性初始条件；并训练一个模拟器（emulator）建立衰变参数与弛豫后密度剖面之间的映射关系。

Result: 发现小反冲速度下晕保持束缚但显著膨胀；各向异性初条件对最终密度剖面有不可忽略影响；成功构建了衰变参数到密度剖面的高效映射模型。

Conclusion: 衰变暗物质在晕尺度上产生可观测的结构特征，这些特征可与现有宇宙学限制互补，并有望被下一代天文巡天实验检验。

Abstract: We study the evolution of self-gravitating spherical dark matter halos composed of dark matter particles that decay to a slightly less massive particle and a massless particle. The small kick velocity imparted to the daughter particle upon decay heats the halo. If the kick velocity is small compared with the characteristic velocity, the halo remains bound but puffs out. We also consider, for the first time, decaying halos with anisotropic initial conditions and find non-negligible effects on the resulting density profile. To simulate the halo evolution, we utilize the recently developed \texttt{NSphere} code across a broad range of decay times and kick velocities. We train an emulator to provide a mapping between the decay parameters and the relaxed density profile. We discuss how the resulting halo-scale signatures complement existing cosmological constraints on decaying dark matter and can be tested by upcoming surveys.

</details>

Vielzeuf,S. Vinciguerra,M. von Wietersheim-Kramsta,N. A. Walton,L. Wang,A. H. Wright,H. W. Yeung*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用MAMBO模拟和Euclid官方数据处理流程，评估Euclid宽场与深场光谱巡天在DR3深度下对恒星形成主序（MS）、尘埃质量关系（DMR）和质量-金属丰度关系（MZR）的重建能力，发现深场在多数红移范围内能更完整、低偏倚地采样这些关系，而宽场则存在显著选择效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估Euclid光谱巡天（尤其是DR3深度下的宽场EWS和深场EDS）在观测发射线星系时，能否准确恢复关键星系演化关系——恒星形成主序（MS）、尘埃质量关系（DMR）和质量-金属丰度关系（MZR）。

Method: 基于MAMBO（博洛尼亚丰度匹配模拟）生成的光谱模拟数据，并经Euclid官方处理流程分析；使用Paβ、Hα和[OII]作为SFR示踪器，结合Balmer减幅推导消光和尘埃质量，利用多条光学静止帧发射线（如Hα、Hβ、[OIII]、[NII]）估算气体金属丰度。

Result: EWS因较浅的极限星等导致MS高估归一化并压平斜率；EDS在z≤1.88、M*≥10.3 M⊙时可近乎完整采样Paβ/Hα定义的MS；[OII] MS在高z时系统性偏向高SFR；DMR通过Balmer减幅在z≈0.9–1.88可靠恢复，但EWS受限于Hβ探测而偏向低消光星系；'golden sample'在z≈1.5–1.9可实现~0.1 dex金属丰度精度（M*≥10 M⊙），但低质量星系偏差达0.15 dex；该样本相对H波段选源更倾向上主序和下MZR。

Conclusion: Euclid深场巡天在重建MS、DMR和MZR方面具有明显优势，尤其在中低红移和较高恒星质量区间表现稳健；宽场受选择效应影响较大；当前模拟虽忽略光谱污染和探测器残留等现实效应，但仍为后续Euclid光谱数据分析提供了重要基准。

Abstract: The Euclid spectroscopic surveys will substantially increase the number of observed emission-line galaxies. We assess the ability of the Euclid Wide Survey (EWS) and Euclid Deep Survey (EDS), at the final DR3 depth, to recover the star-forming main sequence (MS), dust-mass relation (DMR), and mass-metallicity relation (MZR) from emission-line measurements. We use MAMBO (mocks with abundance matching in Bologna) spectroscopic simulations processed with the official Euclid pipelines. The MS is traced over $0\leq z\leq 4$ using Pa$β$, H$α$, and [OII] as SFR tracers. The shallow EWS flux limit biases the MS towards higher normalisation and a flatter slope by selecting the brightest, most strongly star-forming galaxies. The deeper EDS enables almost complete sampling with Pa$β$ and H$α$ at log$_{10}(M_*/M_\odot)\geq10.3$ over $0\leq z\leq 1.88$, with mild biases at both redshift limits. The [OII]-based MS is increasingly biased towards higher SFRs at the highest redshifts. The DMR is traced through the Balmer decrement over $1.45\leq z\leq 1.88$ and $0.9\leq z\leq 1.88$ in the EWS and EDS, respectively. The derived colour excess agree well with the input values, although detecting the faint H$β$ line restricts the sample to less obscured galaxies at fixed $M_*$, particularly in the EWS; the DMR is better sampled in the EDS. Finally, a golden sample detected in multiple optical rest-frame emission lines at $1.49\leq z\leq 1.88$ recovers gas-phase metallicities with typical accuracy of 0.1 dex at log$_{10}(M_*/M_\odot)\geq10$, while lower-mass galaxies show biases up to 0.15 dex. Relative to H-band limited sample, this sample preferentially traces the upper MS and lower MZR. Despite the simplified simulations lacking effects such as mutual spectral contamination and detector persistence, these forecasts provide a benchmark for future Euclid spectroscopic analyses.

</details>

Walton,L. Wang,A. H. Wright*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文预测了欧几里得（Euclid）任务宽视场与深视场巡天中多种发射线（如[OII]、Hα、[OIII]等）星系的探测数量，比较了不同模型的预测差异，并通过MAMBO光锥与FastSpec模拟光谱，结合官方光谱处理流程评估了红移和流量测量精度；结果表明Hα发射体预测较稳健，而氧线预测模型依赖性强，总体将极大拓展z>2的宇宙学与星系演化研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持Euclid任务的科学目标，需提前预测其在宽视场（EWS）和深视场（EDS）巡天中对关键发射线星系（如[OII]、Hα、[OIII]等）的探测能力，以评估其在高红移大尺度结构和重子声学振荡（BAO）研究中的潜力。

Method: 采用多种模型（包括半解析模型和经验标度关系）预测发射线星系数密度；基于MAMBO光锥，用FastSpec快速生成Euclid观测条件下的1D模拟光谱；再输入Euclid官方光谱处理流程，提取红移与谱线流量，并校正流量损失与红移误判效应。

Result: 预测Euclid将在EWS中探测约4200万Hα发射体、130万[OII]和750万[OIII]发射体；在EDS中探测约170万Hα、50万[OII]和80万[OIII]发射体；Hα+[NII]预测一致性好（相对RMS~19%），而[OII]和[OIII]预测离散度大（~44%和41%），反映模型依赖性强；经校正后，流量测量可较好恢复本征计数，尤其在EDS中。

Conclusion: Euclid将提供迄今最大规模的高红移发射线星系样本，显著推动z>2的宇宙学与星系演化研究；但非Hα谱线（尤其是氧线）的预测仍受模型不确定性主导，需结合多模型与观测交叉验证。

Abstract: The Euclid mission will revolutionize our understanding of the visible Universe by collecting images and spectra of millions of galaxies across cosmic time and environments. Here, we forecast the number counts of galaxies emitting [OII], H\b{eta}, [OIII], Hα and Pa\b{eta} lines in the Euclid Wide Survey (EWS) and Deep Survey (EDS). We compare predictions from different models, including semi-analytic models and empirical scaling relations. We then simulate spectra for galaxies in the MAMBO lightcone using FastSpec, designed to rapidly generate one-dimensional spectra as observed by Euclid. We process these simulated spectra with the official Euclid spectroscopic pipeline to recover redshifts and line fluxes and derive expected counts. Predictions for Hα+[NII] emitters are broadly consistent across mocks and with existing observations. In the EWS, relative RMS scatter among mock predictions for integrated counts is approximately 19% for Hα+[NII], 44% for [OII] and 41% for [OIII], reflecting greater model dependence for oxygen-line forecasts. After accounting for flux losses and redshift misidentification, corrected flux measurements recover most intrinsic line counts, especially in the EDS. From the MAMBO-based FastSpec simulations, we predict that Euclid will detect about 42 million Hα emitters in the EWS and 1.7 million in the EDS. The same simulations predict approximately 0.5 and 0.8 million [OII] and [OIII] emitters, respectively, in the EDS, and about 1.3 and 7.5 million in the EWS. These non-Hα forecasts remain subject to substantial model-dependent uncertainties, as shown by scatter among the different mocks. These samples will be instrumental in pushing large-scale structure and baryon acoustic oscillation studies beyond redshift z~2, extending Euclid cosmological and galaxy evolution studies beyond what is achievable with Hα alone.

</details>


### [106] [The M87 Black Hole Mass from JWST and Keck Stellar Kinematics Spanning 10 pc to 10 kpc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05486)
*Emily R. Liepold,Chung-Pei Ma,Refa M. Al-Amri,Jonelle L. Walsh*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文结合JWST NIRSpec和Keck KCWI观测，构建了M87星系从约10 pc到10 kpc的连续二维恒星运动学图，并利用修正后的光谱和三轴Schwarzschild轨道建模，精确测得其中心超大质量黑洞质量为(5.38±0.13±0.22)×10⁹ M⊙，统计误差减小2–3倍，其质量与距离比值与EHT结果一致，可作为未来EHT研究的独立先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高M87中心超大质量黑洞质量测量精度，克服JWST NIRSpec光谱中‘wiggle’伪影干扰，并提供与事件视界望远镜（EHT）结果一致且独立的黑洞参数约束。

Method: 开发hyphen算法校正NIRSpec光谱中的周期性‘wiggle’伪影；结合JWST与Keck数据构建跨三个数量级半径的恒星运动学图；采用TriOS代码进行三轴Schwarzschild轨道建模，拟合约5000个运动学约束。

Result: 测得M87黑洞质量为M_BH = (5.38 ± 0.13 ± 0.22) × 10⁹ M⊙，统计误差较先前KCWI单独分析降低2–3倍；质量-距离比θ_g = GM_BH/(c²D) = 3.16 ± 0.08 μas，其90%可信区间与EHT多历元分析结果重叠。

Conclusion: 本工作实现了迄今最精确的M87黑洞质量动力学测量之一，验证了JWST在近邻星系核球动力学研究中的关键能力，并为EHT提供稳健、独立的先验参数。

Abstract: We combine JWST NIRSpec and Keck KCWI observations to construct a contiguous 2D map of M87's stellar kinematics spanning three decades in projected radius, from ${\sim}10$ pc to ${\sim}10$ kpc, and measure the mass of its central supermassive black hole. The innermost stellar kinematic measurements probe projected radii as small as ${\sim}3$% of the black hole's sphere of influence radius, or approximately 30,000 Schwarzschild radii. The NIRSpec spectra within $R < 0.45''$ are contaminated by quasiperiodic "wiggle" artifacts. We develop hyphen, which uses detector spectral traces to predict how the wiggle frequency varies with wavelength, then fits and subtracts the wiggle pattern from each spectrum in each exposure before combining the data. The cleaned spectra reveal a pronounced central rise in stellar velocity dispersion and $h_4$. Triaxial Schwarzschild stellar orbit modeling with the TriOS code, fitting ${\sim}$5,000 kinematic constraints across 618 spatial bins from JWST and Keck, yields a black hole mass of $M_{\mathrm{BH}}=(5.38 \pm 0.13\pm 0.22)\times10^9\,M_\odot$. This result is consistent with our prior KCWI-only determination but with the statistical uncertainty reduced by a factor of 2-3, now subdominant to the systematic uncertainty in the distance ($D$) to M87. Our inferred mass-to-distance ratio is $θ_g=GM_{\mathrm{BH}}/(c^2 D) = 3.16 \pm 0.08\, μ$as, whose 90% credible interval overlaps with that obtained from the multi-epoch Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) analysis, providing a robust and independent prior for future EHT studies.

</details>


### [107] [A pocket of oxygen in a near-pristine galaxy at z=4.2: chemical inhomogeneity at 10-parsec scales in LAP2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05487)
*M. Messa,E. Vanzella,S. Rojas-Ruiz,T. Treu,P. Bergamini,G. Di Rosa,M. Lombardi,P. Rosati,A. Zanella,A. Adamo,A. Bolamperti,F. Calura,M. Castellano,R. Gilli,C. Grillo,U. Meštrić*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST观测了一个z=4.2、强引力透镜放大的极低金属丰度星系LAP2，发现其金属丰度低于0.5%太阳丰度，是目前已知金属丰度最低的星系之一；其核心区域金属丰度略高，可能源于局部自增丰或更高电离参数；该星系质量极小（约10⁵ M⊙），类似大质量星团，且位于低密度环境，支持再电离后低密度区仍可维持近原始气体的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在约束LAP2（一个z=4.2、强透镜放大的极贫氧星系）的氧丰度、电离特性及空间结构，拓展对极贫金属星系（EMPGs）在低质量、高红移端的认知。

Method: 结合JWST/NIRSpec IFU PRISM光谱（~13.6小时）与NIRCam成像数据，对LAP2进行空间分辨光谱分析，测量Hα、[OIII]λ5007、HeIIλ1640等谱线强度、等效宽度、巴耳末衰减及UV斜率，并利用强线标定法估算氧丰度。

Result: LAP2本征极暗（M_UV=−12.7）、质量极小（~10⁵ M⊙），呈致密核（R_eff~7 pc）+弥散包层结构；无尘、年轻（≤10 Myr）恒星爆发；整体氧丰度上限12+log(O/H)≤6.4（<0.5% Z⊙），核心处达~6.6（~1% Z⊙）；HeII未排除无金属星贡献；位于低密度区。

Conclusion: LAP2是迄今确认的质量最小、最暗的极贫金属星系之一，其存在表明低密度环境可在再电离后长期保留近原始气体，为研究早期星系化学演化与第一代恒星提供了关键样本。

Abstract: We aim to constrain the oxygen abundance, the ionizing properties, and the spatial structure of LAP2, a strongly lensed (with a magnification $μ$~50) oxygen-deficient galaxy at z=4.2 behind the galaxy cluster Abell 2744. We present new JWST/NIRSpec IFU PRISM observations (~13.6 hr) of LAP2, which we combine with archival JWST/NIRCam imaging. LAP2 is intrinsically extremely faint (M_UV=-12.7) and has a low stellar mass (M~$10^5~M_\odot$), comparable to that of massive star clusters. It consists of a compact core ($R_{eff}$~7 pc) embedded in a more diffuse emission (with $R_{eff}$~20 pc). Strong H$α$ emission (EW$_{\rm rest}\simeq880$ Å, $\logξ_{\rm ion}\simeq25.3$), a blue UV slope ($β_{\rm UV}\simeq-2.5$, down to $\simeq-3.1$ in the core), and a Balmer decrement consistent with no dust attenuation indicate a young ($\lesssim10$ Myr), dust-free burst. [OIII]$λ$5007 is not detected in the integrated spectrum, implying [OIII]/H$β\leq0.61$ ($3σ$) and, using an extrapolated strong-line calibration, $12+\log({\rm O/H})\leq6.4$ ($\lesssim0.5\%\,Z_\odot$). This places LAP2 among the most metal-poor galaxies known. Faint [OIII] emission is, however, detected in the core (with $\rm4σ$ significance), where we derive $12+\log({\rm O/H})\simeq6.6$ ($\sim1\%\,Z_\odot$). This difference can be explained either by local self-enrichment, requiring only $\rm \sim0.2\,M_\odot$ of newly synthesized oxygen, or by a higher ionization parameter in the core. The upper limit on HeII$λ$1640 (EW$_{\rm rest}<39$ Å, $3σ$) does not exclude a contribution from metal-free stars. LAP2 extends the census of spectroscopically confirmed EMPGs to star-cluster masses and to $M_{\rm UV}\approx-13$. Its location in a low-density region supports a scenario in which underdense environments preserve nearly pristine gas well after reionization.

</details>


### [108] [She Sees Shells by the Stellar Shore: A Kinematic View of NGC 253's Stellar Halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05493)
*Katya Gozman,Eric F. Bell,Richard D'Souza,Mario Mateo,John I. Bailey,Antonela Monachesi,Camila Beltrand*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用Magellan望远镜M2FS光谱仪，首次对NGC 253星系晕中89颗分辨恒星进行了恒星运动学测量，识别出吸积成分（西南壳层）与原位形成成分，并给出其视向速度中值，为未来大口径望远镜研究邻近星系晕结构铺路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邻近星系的恒星晕是古代并合事件的化石记录，蕴含其形成与演化历史；但除本星系群外，对类似银河系质量星系晕中分辨恒星开展光谱观测极具挑战。

Method: 利用麦哲伦望远镜M2FS多目标光纤光谱仪，获取NGC 253西南壳层区域225颗恒星的中等分辨率光谱，从中提取89颗晕星的视向速度，并通过运动学分析区分吸积与原位成分。

Result: 首次测得NGC 253晕中分辨恒星的运动学：吸积成分（西南壳层）中值视向速度为−34_{−12}^{+16} km/s，原位成分中值为167_{−14}^{+16} km/s；同时识别出多个子结构并划分吸积区与原位区。

Conclusion: 恒星运动学是有效区分星系晕中吸积与原位恒星的关键手段；本工作验证了在本星系群外开展此类观测的可行性，为下一代30米级望远镜和宽视场多目标光谱巡天提供了重要先导范例。

Abstract: The stellar halos of nearby galaxies are fossil records of ancient mergers, providing insight into the formation and growth of these systems. These halos often contain substructure in the form of streams, shells, and debris fields that make up dynamically young remnants of past accretions. These remnants can be probed with resolved-star spectroscopic observations, giving insight into the kinematics of bygone satellite companions. Such observations have been made for the Milky Way (MW) and Andromeda, but are much more challenging and expensive to do outside the Local Group. The Local Volume has an abundance of MW-mass galaxies with richly structured halos, such as NGC 253. Leveraging the power of Magellan's M2FS fiber-fed multiobject spectrograph, we have obtained medium-resolution spectra of 225 stars centered on NGC 253's southwestern shell. These spectra enabled us to make the first-ever measurement of the resolved-star stellar kinematics for 89 stars in NGC 253's halo. We also detect substructure in several ways and identify regions likely to be accreted and in-situ, finding among them a low-velocity region coincident with the previously reported SW shell we believe was accreted recently, and an in-situ component that traces the outer disk. After subtracting NGC 253's systemic velocity, we measure median line-of-sight velocities of $-34_{-12}^{+16}$ km/s and $167_{-14}^{+16}$ km/s for the accreted and in-situ populations, respectively. This work highlights the utility of kinematics to differentiate between accreted and in-situ stars, and acts as a pathfinder for future observations with next-generation 30 m telescopes and wide-field multiobject spectrographs.

</details>


### [109] [ReveaLLAGN 2: Exploring the Nuclear Gas and Stellar Kinematics of Nearby LLAGNs with JWST/NIRSpec IFU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05496)
*Antoine Dumont,Nadine Neumayer,Anil C. Seth,Torsten Böker,Jonelle L. Walsh,Micheal Eracleous,Luis C. Ho,Kameron Goold,David Ohlson,Timothy A. Davis*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST/NIRSpec数据研究了八个近邻低光度活动星系核（LLAGN）的核区恒星与气体运动学及空间分布，发现恒星旋转缓慢且速度弥散向中心黑洞增大，而气体运动常与恒星解耦、并与射电喷流方向对齐；电离机制以AGN光电离为主，激波加热仅在较高爱丁顿比时显著；高电离冠状线仅在最高爱丁顿比源中实现空间分辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLAGN是本地宇宙中最常见的黑洞吸积模式，但其在秒差距尺度上的核结构、恒星与气体运动学仍缺乏约束。

Method: 基于JWST/NIRSpec观测八个代表性LLAGN（ReveaLLAGN样本），分析恒星、分子氢、氢复合线及高电离冠状线的运动学与空间分布，并关联其与爱丁顿比的关系。

Result: 恒星视向速度图显示所有目标核区均为缓慢旋转盘，h3与速度呈反相关；速度弥散普遍向中心上升，M104和M94呈现多成分复杂结构；气体运动常与恒星解耦，且常沿射电喷流方向展宽；电离以AGN光电离为主，激波加热仅在高爱丁顿比时重要；冠状线在核光谱中普遍存在，但仅在NGC5128和NGC4395中获得空间分辨运动学。

Conclusion: LLAGN核区恒星动力学相对规则，而气体运动更易受当前吸积态与喷流影响，表明恒星势场与瞬时吸积过程解耦；高电离气体的空间分辨需足够高的爱丁顿比，揭示了LLAGN物理状态随吸积率变化的关键规律。

Abstract: Low-luminosity active galactic nuclei (LLAGN) represent the most common mode of black hole accretion in the local Universe, yet their nuclear structure, stellar and gas kinematics remain poorly constrained at parsec scales. We investigate the nuclear regions of eight nearby representative LLAGN, the ReveaLLAGN sample: M87, M104, M94, M81, NGC1052, NGC4258, NGC5128 and NGC4395. Using JWST/NIRSpec data, we probe the stellar, molecular hydrogen, hydrogen recombination, and high ionisation coronal lines kinematics and their spatial distribution, exploring how detectability and spatial distributions of these lines vary across different Eddington ratios. The stellar LOSV maps reveal a slowly rotating disk in the nuclei of all our targets, characterised by an anti-correlation with the h3 Gauss-Hermite moment. Across the sample, the stellar velocity dispersion rises toward the central black hole. Only in M104 and M94 does the velocity dispersion display a more intricate pattern that departs from a simple disk configuration, indicating the presence of multiple kinematic components in these galaxies. Across the sample, the gas distribution and kinematics exhibit rotation that is frequently decoupled from the stellar rotation, suggesting that the stellar gravitational potential is decoupled from the current accretion state. The gas kinematics are often visually aligned with the radio jet, presenting broader velocity dispersions along the jet direction. Line-ratio maps indicate that the dominant excitation mechanism is photoionisation by the central AGN, with mechanical heating from shocks becoming important only at higher Eddington ratios. Across the sample, we detect coronal lines in all LLAGN when using nuclear-aperture spectra, and we can obtain spatially resolved kinematics only in NGC5128 and NGC4395, the highest Eddington ratio sources.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用DESI DR2数据中10,036颗遥远晕星（RR Lyrae和蓝水平支星）的6D相空间坐标，结合四种动力学建模方法及基于宇宙学模拟的mock测试，系统评估并校正了方法学假设与银河系非平衡态带来的系统偏差，最终给出银河系80 kpc内质量为6.36^{+0.53}_{-0.48}×10¹¹ M⊙、晕质量M₂₀₀c为1.19^{+0.14}_{-0.12}×10¹² M⊙的联合估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确测定银河系质量对理解其形成演化、暗物质分布及与其他星系比较至关重要；然而以往测量受方法假设、观测选择效应及银河系动力学非平衡影响，存在显著系统偏差，亟需系统性量化与校正。

Method: 采用四种动力学建模方法分析DESI DR2中10,036颗RR Lyrae和BHB晕星的6D相空间数据（距离10–95 kpc），并通过基于高精度宇宙学模拟的mock数据集（包含真实观测误差与选择函数建模）定量评估各方法的系统偏差，进而对实测结果进行偏差校正，并用严谨框架联合不同方法与示踪星种的结果。

Result: 获得80 kpc内银河系质量M(r < 80 kpc) = 6.36^{+0.53}_{-0.48} × 10¹¹ M⊙，晕质量M₂₀₀c = 1.19^{+0.14}_{-0.12} × 10¹² M⊙；RRL与BHB子样本各自给出一致结果，验证了方法稳健性。

Conclusion: 通过mock校准可显著抑制动力学质量估计中的系统偏差；本工作为DESI及其他光谱巡天未来高精度银河系质量测量提供了可靠方法论与基准。

Abstract: We present measurements of the mass of the Milky Way using distant halo stars selected from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) Data Release 2 (DR2). We apply four dynamical modeling methods to 10,036 RR Lyrae (RRL) and blue horizontal branch (BHB) stars with 6D phase space coordinates that span from $\sim$10 to 95 kpc from the Galactic center, the largest samples of their kind. All methods are applied to mock surveys based on cosmological simulations that include generating individual tracer stars, convolving their phase-space coordinates with DESI and Gaia DR3-like uncertainties, and tailored models of the survey selection functions. By comparing the performance on these mocks, we quantify how systematic biases from both methodology assumptions and galaxy disequilibrium impact each estimate. The mock-based systematics, which differ by method and tracer, are used to correct the individual estimates and to derive joint estimates using a rigorous formalism that accounts for per-method biases and residual correlations between methods. We obtain a joint estimate of the mass within 80 kpc to be $M(r < 80 \mathrm{\,kpc}) = 6.36^{+0.53}_{-0.48} \times 10^{11}$ M$_\odot$ and a virial mass estimate of $M_\mathrm{200c} = 1.19^{+0.14}_{-0.12} \times 10^{12}$ M$_\odot$, with similar results when combining only RRL-based or BHB-based measurements. These results provide a roadmap for future DESI efforts, as well as those in other spectroscopic surveys, to reliably constrain the mass of our Galaxy.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文介绍了为DESI第二版数据发布（DR2）关键项目构建的AuriDESI模拟星表，用于通过晕族恒星六维运动学测量银河系质量，并验证相关方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为验证DESI DR2中利用遥远晕星六维运动学测量银河系质量的多种技术，需构建高度保真的模拟观测样本。

Method: 基于Auriga宇宙学模拟生成AuriDESI模拟星表，使其匹配DESI DR2关键项目的实际观测特征，包括目标恒星类型（RR Lyrae和蓝水平支星）、视向速度与自行的观测误差、以及巡天区域内的观测完备性变化。

Result: 构建了与DESI观测数据在统计特性上高度一致的AuriDESI示踪星表，并评估了其与真实数据的吻合程度，提出了可推广至同类模拟工作的改进建议；星表已公开发布于Zenodo。

Conclusion: AuriDESI模拟星表是验证银河系质量测量方法的有效工具，其构建方法和评估框架对今后类似模拟巡天工作具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: The Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) survey is an ongoing campaign to obtain millions of spectra, including for stars within the Milky Way. One of DESI's Key Projects for its second data release (DR2) is to measure the mass of the Milky Way using a variety of techniques applied to six-dimensional kinematics of distant halo stars. We present mock surveys generated from the Auriga cosmological simulations (AuriDESI) that are used to validate these techniques in several companion studies. The AuriDESI tracer catalogues are tailored to reflect key features of the DR2 Key Project dataset, including the selection of RR Lyrae and blue horizontal branch stars, uncertainties on line-of-sight velocities and proper motions, and variation in observational completeness within the survey footprint. We discuss how well the resulting AuriDESI tracer catalogues compare to their observed DESI counterparts, including suggesting improvements that can be generalized to similar mock survey efforts within the community. The AuriDESI tracer catalogues are publicly available on Zenodo.

</details>

*Yanrui Zhou,Jiaxin Han,Wenting Wang,Zhaozhou Li,Alexander H. Riley,Monica Valluri,Amanda Byström,Gustavo E. Medina,Sergey E. Koposov,Ting S. Li,Leandro Beraldo e Silva,Oleg Y. Gnedin,N. Kizhuprakkat,N. Sandford,C. S. Frenk,Binbin Gao,Yiran Wei,J. Aguilar,S. Ahlen,C. Allende Prieto,F. Beutler,D. Bianchi,D. Brooks,A. Carnero Rosell,T. Claybaugh,A. P.
