TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的数据驱动动力学建模方法（emPDF），利用DESI MWS巡天的晕星样本，不依赖示踪体分布函数形式，约束银河系质量轮廓，并结合卫星动力学结果得到更精确的银河系晕参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法常假设示踪体分布函数形式，可能引入系统偏差；需发展不依赖先验分布、能处理观测误差与非稳态效应的稳健质量建模方法。

Method: 采用经验分布函数（emPDF）方法，基于DESI MWS中BHB和RR Lyrae晕星数据；通过AuriDESI真实模拟数据校准非稳态偏差与观测误差影响。

Result: BHB星给出log M₂₀₀c = 12.05⁺⁰·²¹₋₀·₁₂，log c₂₀₀c = 1.20⁺⁰·¹¹₋₀·₁₆；RR Lyrae给出log M₂₀₀c = 12.15⁺⁰·²²₋₀·₁₄，log c₂₀₀c = 1.16⁺⁰·⁰⁹₋₀·₁₂；联合卫星动力学后得最终结果：log M₂₀₀c = 12.12±0.05，log c₂₀₀c = 1.16⁺⁰·⁰⁵₋₀·₀₃，相关系数ρ = −0.90。

Conclusion: emPDF方法有效克服了对示踪体分布函数的依赖，两种晕星示踪体结果一致，联合卫星动力学后显著提升了银河系晕质量与浓度参数的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: We employ a novel data-driven dynamical modeling method, the empirical distribution function (emPDF), to constrain the Milky Way mass profile using a large sample of halo stars from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument Milky Way Survey (DESI MWS). The method does not assume any functional form of the tracer distribution. Deviations from the steady-state assumption and the effects of observational errors are further tested and calibrated using a number of realistic mock observations (AuriDESI). Applying the calibrated method to two classes of tracers in DESI, we derive a best-constrained virial mass and concentration of $\log M_\mathrm{200c}/M_{\odot}=12.05_{-0.12}^{+0.21}(_{-0.18}^{+0.26})$ and $\log c_\mathrm{200c}=1.20_{-0.16}^{+0.11}(_{-0.24}^{+0.20})$ for BHBs. For RR Lyrae stars we derive $\log M_\mathrm{200c}/M_{\odot}=12.15_{-0.14}^{+0.22}(_{-0.19}^{+0.26})$ and $\log c_\mathrm{200c}=1.16_{-0.12}^{+0.09}(_{-0.18}^{+0.16})$. The uncertainties quoted outside and within brackets are statistical and total errors including systematic errors calibrated from AuriDESI, respectively. The two tracer populations provide overall consistent measurements of the MW halo mass profile. Combining the halo star constraints with the independent constraint from satellite dynamics, we derive a robust and precise final measurement of the MW halo parameters of $\log M_\mathrm{200c}/M_{\odot}=12.12_{-0.05}^{+0.05}(_{-0.04}^{+0.06})$ and $\log c_\mathrm{200c}=1.16_{-0.03}^{+0.05}(_{-0.07}^{+0.05})$, with correlation coefficients of $ρ=-0.90(-0.54)$.

</details>


### [113] [Quiescent galaxies at cosmic noon: from individual element abundances to star formation histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06331)
*M. Longhetti,F. R. Ditrani,D. Bevacqua,G. De Lucia,A. Gallazzi,A. Gargiulo,F. La Barbera,C. Maraston,D. Thomas,P. Franzetti,C. Mancini,L. Scholz-Díaz,S. Zibetti*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出利用40米级望远镜（如配备SHARP-NEXUS与MORFEO-MCAO系统的ELT）开展z~2–3宇宙正午时期大质量宁静星系的详细化学丰度研究，以突破当前中红外光谱观测在信噪比和空间分辨率上的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大质量宁静星系的形成与早期演化对构建完整的星系形成宇宙学图景至关重要；而其单元素丰度可揭示恒星形成与并合历史的时间尺度和物理条件。目前z~2–3的研究极为匮乏，受限于目标星系的暗弱与致密特性。

Method: 提出基于静止框架光学吸收线的全元素丰度测量方案，论证需S/N~20/分辨元及~0.1角秒空间分辨率，并指出仅40米级望远镜结合SHARP-NEXUS与MORFEO-MCAO自适应光学系统可满足要求；同时建议通过星系过密度区靶向与星系巡天协同实现多目标观测。

Result: 确认SHARP-NEXUS是目前唯一可行的ELT仪器，能同时提供所需灵敏度与角分辨率；并证明通过与高红移星团、4MOST等多路复用设施协同，可高效开展该类稀有目标的化学增丰研究。

Conclusion: 该研究将开启对宇宙最早期大质量星系化学增丰历史的直接探测新窗口，填补z~2–3关键红shift区间观测空白，推动星系形成理论发展。

Abstract: Understanding the formation and early evolution of massive quiescent galaxies is crucial for a complete picture of galaxy formation in a cosmological context. Such a comprehensive picture can be obtained through measurements of individual elemental abundances, which encode the timescales and physical conditions of their star-formation and assembly histories. While extensive work at low and intermediate redshift has revealed strong correlations between stellar mass, metallicity, and abundance ratios, studies at z ~ 2-3 remain extremely limited. Their faintness and compactness make medium-resolution near-infrared spectroscopy for detailed chemical analysis extremely challenging. We present a science case to measure full elemental abundance patterns in a representative sample of massive quiescent galaxies at cosmic noon, exploiting rest-frame optical absorption features. We show that deriving robust stellar population constraints for these systems requires spectra with S/N ~ 20 per resolution element, together with a spatial resolution comparable to their ~ 0.1 arcsec sizes. We show that such requirements can only be met with a 40m class telescope equipped with a powerful adaptive optics system. In this context, SHARP-NEXUS, in combination with MORFEO-MCAO capabilities, emerges as the only viable ELT instrument capable of enabling this science, providing both the sensitivity and angular resolution needed. Although massive quiescent galaxies are rare at these redshifts, their study can efficiently exploit the instrument's multiplexing by targeting galaxy overdensities and coordinating with surveys of star-forming galaxies. Such synergistic strategies, proven feasible in high-redshift clusters and by multiplex-sharing facilities like 4MOST, would allow SHARP-NEXUS to open a new window on the chemical enrichment of the earliest massive galaxies.

</details>


### [114] [A distribution-function estimate of the Milky Way mass profile from outer halo stars in DESI DR2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05521)
*Gustavo E. Medina,Thomas M. Callingham,Monica Valluri,Alexander H. Riley,Ting S. Li,Jiaxin Han,Leandro Beraldo e Silva,Oleg Y. Gnedin,Amanda Byström,Sergey E. TL;DR: 本文利用DESI DR2数据，通过蓝水平分支星（BHB）和RR Lyrae星（RRL）测量银河系质量，采用分层贝叶斯方法GME并校正系统偏差，得到80 kpc内质量分别为0.64和0.53×10¹² M⊙，并讨论了不同示踪体和方法带来的系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定银河系质量对理解其形成演化、暗物质分布及与卫星星系相互作用至关重要；此前基于DESI DR1的测量受限于样本大小，需借助更大样本（DR2）提升精度并评估系统误差。

Method: 使用DESI DR2中279颗BHB星和213颗RRL星（距离银心约50–80 kpc），结合分层贝叶斯框架GME（假设幂律势与示踪体密度分布，并计入距离与速度误差）；通过24个AuriDESI模拟星表验证并校正约15%的质量高估偏差。

Result: 80 kpc内质量：BHB为0.64⁺⁰·⁰⁶₋₀·₀₆×10¹² M⊙，RRL为0.53⁺⁰·⁰⁵₋₀·₀₅×10¹² M⊙；含人马座星流后降低约10%；外推至100 kpc及锚定NFW晕得M₂₀₀分别为1.12⁺⁰·¹⁴₋₀·₁₂和0.89⁺⁰·¹¹₋₀·₁₀×10¹² M⊙；BHB结果系统性高于RRL。

Conclusion: 示踪体选择（BHB vs RRL）显著影响质量估计结果，凸显系统误差评估的重要性；本工作是DESI DR2关键项目的一部分，旨在多样本、多方法联合约束银河系质量及其不确定性。

Abstract: We present measurements of the Milky Way mass using blue horizontal-branch (BHB) and RR Lyrae (RRL) stars from the second data release of DESI (DR2), covering its first three years of operations. We employ 279 BHBs and 213 RRLs at $\sim\!50$--$80$\,kpc from the Galactic center, selected from catalogs roughly twice as large as those of DESI DR1, used in our previous mass measurement. We apply the Galactic Mass Estimator (GME), a hierarchical Bayesian framework that assumes power-law forms for the potential and the tracer density, and accounts for distance and velocity uncertainties. Validating the method on 24 AuriDESI mock catalogs (six Auriga halos at four viewing angles), we find that it overestimates the enclosed mass by $\sim\!15\%$ at 80\,kpc, and correct our results accordingly. We obtain $M(<80\,{\rm kpc}) = 0.64^{+0.06}_{-0.06}$ and $0.53^{+0.05}_{-0.05} \times 10^{12}\,M_\odot$ for BHBs and RRLs, respectively, while including Sagittarius stream stars lowers these masses by $\sim\!10$\%. Extrapolating to 100\,kpc, we obtain $M(<100\,{\rm kpc}) = 0.74^{+0.07}_{-0.07}$ and $0.62^{+0.06}_{-0.07} \times 10^{12}\,M_\odot$, and anchoring an NFW halo to our GME constraints at 65\,kpc yields virial masses of $M_{200} = 1.12^{+0.14}_{-0.12}$ and $0.89^{+0.11}_{-0.10} \times 10^{12}\,M_\odot$. BHBs yield systematically higher masses than RRLs across the full radial range, reflecting the impact of tracer choice even within a single method. This work is part of a wider DESI DR2 Key Project effort to measure the Milky Way mass and constrain systematics arising from different samples and methodologies.

</details>


### [115] [Identifying Compact Object Candidates using Gaia DR3 Astrometric Accelerations and Jerks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05536)
*Andrew Kinnion,Jeff J. Andrews,Eirini Kasdagli*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Gaia DR3 9参数非单星天体测量解识别可能含有大质量致密伴星（如黑洞）的双星系统的新方法，通过建模不同轨道类型下的投影加速度（$\dot{\mu}$）和急动度（$\ddot{\mu}$），结合模拟观测偏差筛选，验证了该方法的有效性，并以HD 12871等目标为例进行了应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gaia DR3提供了大量具有非线性自行（即存在加速度/急动度信号）的恒星数据，但其中7参数和9参数解尚未被系统用于搜寻致密天体伴星；现有研究多聚焦于已得轨道解的目标，而大量仅具加速度/急动度信号的源潜力未被充分挖掘。

Method: 构建四类双星模型（倾斜偏心、面朝偏心、面朝圆轨、POSYDON生成的真实BH+亮星模型），推导其在天球上的解析加速度与急动度表达式；利用gaiamock模拟Gaia观测偏差，筛选能被Gaia以9参数成功拟合的系统；建立基于$\dot{\mu}$–$\ddot{\mu}$联合分布的致密伴星候选体甄别方法。

Result: 证实即使9参数拟合无法完全约束完整轨道，仍可通过加速度与急动度的联合特征有效识别含大质量致密伴星（尤其是黑洞）的双星候选体；该方法成功应用于HD 12871及类Gaia BH3系统分析。

Conclusion: Gaia DR3的9参数天体测量解是搜寻未分辨致密伴星（特别是黑洞）的重要新探针；本文提出的基于$\dot{\mu}$和$\ddot{\mu}$的物理建模与偏差校正方法，为未来大规模致密天体普查提供了可靠框架。

Abstract: Gaia Data Release 3 (DR3) provides astrometric measurements for more than a billion sources, hundreds of thousands of which have non-single-star astrometric solutions that indicate deviations from linear proper motion. Although sources with orbital solutions have been extensively inspected for compact objects via the astrometric mass function, two other solution types--the 7-parameter constant-acceleration solutions characterized by $\dot{\mathbfμ}$, and the 9-parameter variable-acceleration solutions characterized by $\dot{\mathbfμ}$ and $\ddot{\mathbfμ}$--have not been as carefully explored. In this work, we develop a methodology for selecting sources within the Gaia DR3 9-parameter non-single-star astrometric solutions that are most likely to host massive compact companions. We assume that a source is the luminous star in a binary orbit around a dark object and derive analytic expressions for the sky-projected accelerations and jerks for three binary populations with different orbit approximations: inclined eccentric, face-on eccentric, and face-on circular. Using POSYDON, we develop a fourth, more realistic population of binaries, each consisting of a luminous star and a black hole. For each population, we address observational biases using gaiamock and select only the systems it fits with a 9-parameter solution. We find that binaries likely to host massive compact companions can be identified from the characteristic joint astrometric accelerations and jerks exhibited by the luminous stars, despite 9-parameter fits under-constraining full orbits. Finally, to illustrate the utility of our approach, we analyze a historical object of interest, HD 12871, and examine 9-parameter fits of Gaia BH3-like binaries.

</details>


### [116] [Revisiting the Stellar-Dynamical Mass of the Central Black Hole in NGC 3227](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05547)
*Nabanita Das,Misty C. Bentz,Eugene Vasiliev,Monica Valluri,Christopher A. Onken*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用NIFS光谱数据和HST图像，结合Schwarzschild轨道叠加法（FORSTAND代码），测得NGC 3227中心黑洞质量为(1.2±0.5)×10⁷ M⊙，精度优于以往恒星动力学测量，并与反响映射及气体动力学结果一致，强调多方法交叉校准对黑洞质量标度的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同方法测得的超大质量黑洞质量需交叉校准以统一标度、识别并减小各方法的系统误差与偏差。

Method: 基于Gemini/NIFS的K波段空间分辨恒星光谱提取视线速度分布（LOSVD），结合HST/WFC3图像建模表面亮度（四成分），采用开源代码FORSTAND实施Schwarzschild轨道叠加法构建恒星动力学模型。

Result: 得到NGC 3227中心黑洞质量最佳拟合值为(1.2±0.5)×10⁷ M⊙；该结果与前人恒星动力学、反响映射结果一致，与气体动力学结果在2σ内相符。

Conclusion: 高信噪比LOSVD高阶矩约束提升了恒星动力学黑洞质量测量精度；多方法结果一致性支持当前黑洞质量测量体系的可靠性，凸显跨技术比较对系统误差控制的关键作用。

Abstract: We present a new stellar-dynamical mass for the supermassive black hole in the nearby Seyfert galaxy NGC 3227. We determined the line-of-sight velocity distributions of stars in the nuclear region of NGC 3227 using archival spatially-resolved $K$-band spectra obtained with NIFS at Gemini Observatory. The surface brightness profile of the galaxy is described by four components, measured from a WFC3 F547M image from the Hubble Space Telescope. We constructed dynamical models using the Schwarzschild orbit-superposition method implemented in the open-source code $\texttt{FORSTAND}$. We find a best-fit black hole mass of $M_{\mathrm{BH}}$=$(1.2\pm0.5) \times 10^7$ $M_{\odot}$. This mass is consistent with a previous mass from R. I. Davies et al. based on stellar dynamics using orbit-based modeling, but our results make use of higher signal-to-noise data from which the higher-order moments of the stellar LOSVDs are constrained, thus providing a more precise $M_{\mathrm{BH}}$. Our result is also consistent with the $M_{\mathrm{BH}}$ constraint from velocity-resolved reverberation mapping reported by M. C. Bentz et al.. And while our best-fit mass is slightly smaller than the mass constraint from gas-dynamical modeling reported by E. K. S. Hicks & M. A. Malkan (after scaling to the same adopted distance), they are within 2$σ$ of each other. Direct comparisons of black hole masses from different techniques are rare but crucial to ensure all black hole masses are on the same scale, and to identify and minimize the uncertainties and possible sources of bias associated with each technique.

</details>


### [117] [The impact of Population III stars on the elemental enrichment of ultra-faint galaxies in the FIRE-3 zoom-in simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05589)
*Pratik J. Gandhi,Earl P. Bellinger,Marla Geha,Andrew Wetzel,Coral Wheeler,Yasmine J. Meziani,Jenna Samuel,Francisco J. Mercado,Jorge Moreno,Philip F. Hopkins*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过FIRE-3宇宙学模拟，研究了星族III（Pop. III）恒星对超微弱矮星系（UFDs）金属丰度演化的影响，发现Pop. III可建立Z_floor ~ 1e-5–1e-3 Zsun的金属丰度下限，但仅靠Pop. III无法系统解释观测到的UFDs质量-金属丰度关系（尤其是[Fe/H] ~ −2.5），需后续Ia型超新星等铁增丰过程；同时预测了[Mg/H]与恒星质量的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测到的本星系群超微弱矮星系（UFDs）的恒星质量-金属丰度关系与多数理论预言存在张力，暗示在低金属丰度下需要修正或补充元素增丰机制。

Method: 采用FIRE-3高分辨率（~30–110 M⊙，0.1–0.5 pc）宇宙学流体动力学zoom-in模拟，构建孤立UFD及LMC质量环境，引入多种星族III（Pop. III）子网格模型（Z < Z_crit时形成8–100 M⊙大质量恒星），系统分析其反馈对恒星形成历史（SFH）和元素丰度（特别是Fe、Mg）的影响。

Result: Pop. III模型在z=0为UFDs设定了有效金属丰度下限（Z_floor ~ 1e-5–1e-3 Zsun，即[Fe/H]_floor ~ −7至−5）；但仅靠Pop. III无法系统提升多数UFDs至观测所需的[Fe/H] ~ −2.5；达到该水平的UFDs均具有延展SFH及显著Ia型超新星铁增丰；Pop. III反馈还影响[Mg/Fe]比和金属丰度分布，并预测了恒星质量–[Mg/H]关系。

Conclusion: 星族III恒星虽能建立金属丰度底限并影响早期化学演化，但不足以单独解决UFDs质量-金属丰度关系的观测–理论张力；后续恒星形成及Ia型超新星等延迟铁增丰过程至关重要；研究为UFDs化学演化提供了更完整的物理图像，并给出可检验的[Mg/H]–质量关系预测。

Abstract: The observed stellar mass-metallicity relation for ultra-faint galaxies in the Local Group (LG) is in tension with many theoretical predictions, suggesting the need for modified or additional channels of elemental enrichment at low metallicities. We present a suite of FIRE-3 cosmological baryonic zoom-in simulations of isolated ultra-faint galaxies run to z=0 (maximum baryonic resolution ~ 30 Msun, 0.1 pc) and LMC-mass environments (maximum baryonic resolution ~ 110 Msun, 0.5 pc), with different sub-grid models for Population III stars. Under our Pop. III model(s), star particles forming below a critical metallicity Z_crit represent an initial mass function (IMF) consisting of only massive stars (8-100 Msun). We examine how various stellar feedback processes impact the star formation histories (SFHs) and elemental abundances of ultra-faint galaxies. We find that our Pop. III model variants introduce an effective metallicity floor in ultra-faints at z=0, at Z_floor ~ 1e-5 Zsun to 1e-3 Zsun or [Fe/H]_floor ~ -7 to -5 depending on the model. However, simply including the Pop. III model does not systematically guarantee that all (or even most) ultra-faints enrich up to [Fe/H] ~ -2.5 to fully resolve the tension with the observed mass-metallicity relation, even though we obtain some galaxies at that level. Crucially, the ultra-faints that do enrich in [Fe/H] to this level observed in the LG have extended SFHs after Pop. III enrichment and significant iron enrichment from white dwarf (Type Ia) supernovae, indicating that iron enrichment from Pop. III alone is not sufficient. We also examine the ways in which early-time Pop. III feedback impacts the metallicity distributions and [Mg/Fe] abundance ratios in ultra-faint galaxies, and make a prediction for the stellar mass - [Mg/H] metallicity relation from our simulations.

</details>


### [118] [Mock CSST NUV-NIR imaging of TNG50 galaxies with resolved stellar populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05875)
*Jing Tang,Ran Li,Yang Chen,Chao Liu,Alessandro Bressan,Léo Girardi,Chengliang Wei,Tianmeng Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成CSST（中国空间站望远镜）类模拟图像的框架，用于从TNG50流体动力学模拟中合成包含分辨恒星、弥散光及尘埃消光的星系图像，并已用两个TNG50星系验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持CSST对分辨恒星群体的观测分析，需构建兼具物理真实性与观测保真度的模拟图像框架，以支撑源探测、测光恢复和完备性研究。

Method: 基于TNG50模拟星系，结合TRILEGAL恒星采样、SKIRT辐射转移建模尘埃消光（含原生云遮蔽）、以及CSST模拟流水线（PSF卷积、天光背景、仪器噪声）生成多波段mock图像。

Result: 成功生成了旋涡星系与矮不规则星系在7个光度波段的CSST级mock图像，完整包含分辨恒星、未分辨弥散光（含星云发射）、尘埃衰减及主要观测效应，并附带精确的天体测量与测光标定信息。

Conclusion: 该框架统一整合了恒星种群合成、尘埃辐射转移与观测模拟，具备良好可扩展性，适用于其他流体模拟与望远镜配置，为CSST恒星天文学提供关键仿真工具。

Abstract: Aims. We aim to develop a framework for producing CSST-like mock images of resolved stellar populations from simulated galaxies, demonstrated here using two TNG50 galaxies as illustrative test cases. Methods. Starting from simulated galaxies from TNG50, we use the population synthesis code TRILEGAL to sample individual stars from star particles, retaining bright stars explicitly and treating the remaining unresolved light, including faint stellar light and nebular emission from star-forming regions, as a diffuse background component of the galaxy. Based on the dust distribution inferred from the gas mass and metallicity of gas cells, we use the radiative transfer (RT) code SKIRT to construct the dust model. From this model, we compute the line-of-sight extinction for individual stars, while image-level transmission maps for the diffuse light are derived through RT simulations. For young stars still embedded in their natal molecular clouds, we additionally include birth-cloud attenuation. The resulting dust-attenuated stellar catalogues and diffuse-light images are then processed with the CSST simulation pipeline, which applies PSF convolution, sky background, and instrumental noise. Results. Our framework produces mock CSST images for both a spiral galaxy and a dwarf irregular galaxy from TNG50 in the seven photometric bands. The mock images combine resolved stars, unresolved diffuse light, and dust attenuation within a unified framework, and include the main observational effects expected for the survey. The images are provided with known astrometric and photometric calibration information, and are intended as realistic image-level inputs for subsequent source detection, photometric recovery, and completeness analyses of resolved stellar populations. The framework is designed to be adaptable to other hydrodynamic simulations and observing configurations.

</details>


### [119] [Spectroscopy-informed Deep Learning Searches for Photometric Little Red Dots in JWST Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06026)
*Chenxuan Zhang,Huanian Zhang,Qingwen Wu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨模态机器学习框架，利用JWST的NIRCam成像与NIRSpec/PRISM光谱数据联合建模，将光谱信息迁移至成像表征，从而仅凭成像即可识别'小红点'（LRDs）；该方法在已知样本中发现35个新候选体，其中10/13具宽Hα发射，并在公开数据中新增数百候选体，揭示了现有颜色选择法可能遗漏的LRD多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LRD样本多依赖各异的颜色选择标准，导致样本差异大、覆盖不全，难以系统挖掘JWST大数据中的真实LRD多样性。

Method: 构建跨模态机器学习框架，融合匹配的JWST/NIRCam成像与NIRSpec/PRISM光谱数据，将光谱特征（如宽Hα）映射为成像空间的可判别表征，实现纯成像驱动的LRD识别。

Result: 在已知高红移星系中识别出35个新LRD候选体，其中10/13具宽Hα；在Dawn档案及另两个JWST巡天中分别新增357和23个候选体；新候选体SED分布更广，超出传统颜色选样范围。

Conclusion: 光谱引导的成像分类是高效挖掘JWST大视场成像数据中新兴天体（如LRDs）的有效范式，可弥补颜色选择法的偏差与局限，为后续系统性研究提供实用框架。

Abstract: Little Red Dots (LRDs) are a population of compact, red sources discovered at high redshift with JWST that exhibit distinctive V-shaped spectral energy distributions (SEDs). Existing LRD samples are typically identified using a variety of color-based selection criteria, which can lead to differences in the populations recovered by different studies. We develop a cross-modal machine-learning framework that combines matched NIRCam imaging and NIRSpec/PRISM spectroscopy to transfer spectroscopic information into imaging-based representations, enabling LRD identification from JWST/NIRCam imaging alone. Applied to spectroscopically observed high-redshift galaxies previously classified as non-LRDs, our model identifies 35 new LRD candidates. The majority satisfy at least one independent LRD color selection criterion, and among the 13 candidates with medium- or high-resolution NIRSpec grating spectra, 10 exhibit broad H$α$ emission - a hallmark of LRDs. We further apply the classifier to public NIRCam imaging from the Dawn JWST Archive and two independent JWST surveys, yielding 357 and 23 additional LRD candidates, respectively. The candidates recovered by our method span a broader range of observed SED properties than those in existing samples, suggesting that current color-selected samples may not fully capture the diversity of LRD-like systems present in JWST surveys. Our findings highlight the value of spectroscopy-informed photometric classification as a powerful strategy for systematically mining large-area JWST imaging datasets and provide a practical framework for future studies of this emerging population.

</details>


### [120] [Aperture-dependent [O III] profile variability: Clues to the nature of double-peaked narrow emission lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06150)
*Qi Zheng,Xingqian Chen,Zhegeng Chen,Xue-Guang Zhang,Qirong Yuan*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于光圈依赖性的方法，通过比较不同巡天项目（SDSS、DESI、LAMOST）对类星体SDSS J0941的多光圈光谱观测，研究双峰窄发射线（DPNELs）的物理起源；发现光圈大小显著影响双峰轮廓，排除了简单AGN并合、外流或轴对称旋转盘模型，支持非轴对称旋转窄线区模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双峰窄发射线（DPNELs）的物理起源尚不明确，传统单光圈光谱难以区分不同空间区域的动力学贡献，亟需一种能利用空间采样差异进行诊断的新方法。

Method: 提出光圈依赖性分析法：利用SDSS（3″）、DESI（1.5″）和LAMOST（3.3″）三种不同光纤口径获取的同一目标光谱，对比其[O III]等DPNELs的轮廓变化（峰位、分离、宽度、通量），结合几何建模检验多种物理模型（双核AGN、外流、旋转盘、非轴对称旋转NLR）。

Result: 不同光圈下DPNELs峰分离、相对通量和宽度呈现系统性差异：小光圈显著削弱红移峰通量与宽度，蓝移峰则相对稳定；该模式无法被双核AGN或轴对称模型解释，仅非轴对称旋转NLR模型可自洽复现全部观测特征。

Conclusion: DPNELs的光圈依赖性是诊断其物理结构的关键探针；多光圈光谱联合分析极大提升了对NLR几何与运动学的分辨能力，但模型简并性仍构成挑战，需更高分辨率空间光谱进一步验证。

Abstract: We propose a simple and practical aperture-dependent method for investigating the physical origin of double-peaked narrow emission lines (DPNELs). Spectroscopic observations obtained from different apertures probe different spatial regions and therefore different gas kinematics within the narrow line region (NLR). Consequently, changes in the profiles of DPNELs observed with different apertures can provide important constraints on their physical origins. We applied this method to SDSS J0941 by comparing spectra obtained from the SDSS, DESI, and LAMOST surveys. Significant differences are found in the double-peaked profiles of the different spectra, demonstrating that aperture effects can strongly influence the integrated DPNEL profiles. The AGN model, where the two nuclei lie in a plane perpendicular to the line of sight, can qualitatively explain the asymmetric sampling of the two NLRs, but it cannot explain the different peak separations of different spectra. The observed asymmetric evolution of the two components disfavors a simple AGN-driven outflow and a rotating disk-like NLR scenario. Of the models considered, only a more complex non-axisymmetric rotating NLR provides a self-consistent explanation for the observed aperture-dependent variations. In this scenario, the blueshifted emitting region is more compact and remains largely enclosed by the smaller aperture, resulting in an increased line width with little change in flux. In contrast, the redshifted emitting region is more spatially extended and is therefore more strongly affected by the reduced aperture, leading to pronounced decreases in both flux and line width. Our results illustrate the diagnostic potential of combining multi-aperture spectroscopic surveys to study DPNEL origins, while highlighting the difficulty of distinguishing between physical scenarios.

</details>


### [121] [Evolutionary Map of the Universe (EMU): Selection of active galactic nuclei and star-formation emission with machine-learning techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06190)
*R. Carvajal,I. Matute,J. Afonso,H. Messias,M. J. I. Brown,D. Farrah,A. M. Hopkins,J. Th. van Loon,K. J. Luken,C. M. Pennock,J. Prathap,S. S. Shabala,D. A. Leahy,S. F. Rahman,K. A. Pimbblet,T. Zafar*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用预训练的多阶段机器学习流水线，对EMU先导巡天（EMU-PS）射电源进行光学至红外测光分类与光度红移估计，显著扩充了AGN与非AGN样本（分别提升15倍和100倍），揭示了射电光度与星系物理过程间的复杂关系，并验证了该方法在大规模巡天（如完整EMU和SKA）中的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期大天区射电巡天（如EMU）产生了海量源，亟需高效、鲁棒的自动分类与物理性质推断方法；传统光谱或颜色选择难以满足规模与深度需求。

Method: 采用预训练的多阶段机器学习流水线，基于EMU-PS源的光学至红外测光数据，进行AGN/非AGN分类与光度红移估计，并据此计算射电光度等物理量。

Result: 将EMU-PS中AGN与非AGN样本分别提升超15倍（z~5内，召回率96%）和近100倍（z>1，召回率94%）；预测完整EMU将获超340万AGN候选体；发现部分低红移非AGN源射电光度超出恒星形成预期；射电光度呈预期演化趋势，但800–1088 MHz谱指数无显著红移演化。

Conclusion: 该ML流程具备高度可扩展性，适用于EMU及未来SKA巡天；多波段联合分析能揭示仅靠颜色截断或光谱无法识别的射电源类群与演化特征。

Abstract: Sources detected by recent large-area radio surveys require robust classification and characterisation. The Evolutionary Map of the Universe (EMU) is a prime example of machine-learning (ML) techniques efficiently enriching existing catalogues with classifications and derived physical properties that would be impractical to obtain from spectroscopy or colour selection alone. We aim to build a labelled sample of sources in the EMU pilot survey (EMU-PS, at 944 MHz, with rms ~ 30 uJy/beam over 270 deg2 in the southern sky) classified as being dominated by active galactic nuclei (AGNs) or non-AGN processes including star-formation. We will then use this sample to examine radio properties and redshift-dependent trends. We applied a pre-trained multi-stage ML pipeline to optical-to-infrared photometry of EMU-PS sources to derive classifications and photometric redshifts, which we used to compute additional radio properties, including luminosities. We increase the known number of AGNs and non-AGNs in the EMU-PS by more than a factor of 15 (recall 96 %) up to z ~ 5, and by nearly a factor of 100 (recall 94 %) for z > 1, respectively. In extrapolating these numbers for the full EMU survey, we expect to recover more than 3.4 million AGN candidates. A non-negligible low-redshift subsample of selected non-AGNs shows radio luminosities in excess of those expected from star formation alone, revealing detachment between photometric and radio classification. Radio-derived properties show expected trends for luminosities and a lack of evolution for in-band (800-1088 MHz) spectral indices. The ML-based pipeline provides a scalable way to produce large catalogues for the ongoing EMU survey and future Square Kilometre Array observations and reveals that multi-wavelength analysis can uncover radio populations and trends often missed by colour cuts or spectroscopy alone.

</details>


### [122] [A brief overview of the baryon cycle in galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06264)
*S. Veronese*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文综述了星系形成与演化中气体在宇宙网、星系周介质（CGM）和星际介质（ISM）之间循环的过程，即重子循环，涵盖气体吸积、恒星形成及反馈调控机制，并指出了当前亟待解决的关键科学问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系形成与演化的物理机制，特别是气体在不同尺度介质间的循环过程及其对星系增长的调控作用。

Method: 文献综述，整合当前关于重子循环的观测、理论与数值模拟研究成果。

Result: 系统梳理了气体从宇宙网吸积进入CGM和ISM、转化为恒星、以及通过反馈返回CGM或外流的完整循环图景，并明确了若干关键未解问题。

Conclusion: 重子循环是理解星系演化的核心框架，未来需依靠更高精度的多波段观测、更真实的宇宙学模拟及理论发展共同推进。

Abstract: Galaxy formation and evolution is governed by the continuous exchange of gas between the cosmic web, the circumgalactic medium (CGM), and the interstellar medium (ISM) of galaxies. This brief review summarises our current understanding of this baryon cycle, from the accretion of gas onto galaxies to its conversion into stars, and the feedback processes that regulate the growth of galaxies. Several open questions will be highlighted, that will encourage progresses in cosmological simulations, multiwavelength observations, and theory to resolve.

</details>


### [123] [Understanding galaxies and the origin of the golden mass through the study of scaling relations across cosmic history](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06333)
*C. Tortora,A. Gallazzi,A. Gargiulo,F. La Barbera,N. R. Napolitano,R. Ragusa,S. Zibetti*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了星系中'黄金质量'（5×10^10 M⊙）的起源与演化，指出其在恒星形成效率、暗物质比例及质量密度梯度等多方面具有关键作用；提出利用ELT上的SHARP仪器，在高红移（z > 1.5）开展空间分辨观测，以追踪该特征质量随宇宙时间的出现过程，并结合模拟厘清并合、超新星与AGN反馈等物理机制的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系中'黄金质量'如何、为何及何时在宇宙演化中出现，尤其在尚未充分探索的高红移（z > 1.5）阶段，以揭示星系质量组装和恒星形成效率演化的物理本质。

Method: 利用欧洲极大望远镜（ELT）上新一代高分辨率积分场光谱仪SHARP，结合多目标积分场单元（mIFU）模式，对z > 1.5星系开展空间分辨观测，测量恒星种群梯度、暗物质分数和总质量密度斜率，并与宇宙学模拟对比分析。

Result: SHARP将提供比JWST更锐利、更深的观测数据，实现对早期和晚型星系上述关键参数的精确测量，从而首次系统追踪'黄金质量'在宇宙时间中的 emergence 过程。

Conclusion: ‘黄金质量’是星系演化中一个普适性标度，其起源与星系内多种反馈过程及暗物质势阱共同塑造的质量组装历史密切相关；SHARP将在高红移观测上发挥不可替代作用，为建立统一的星系演化理论提供关键实证基础。

Abstract: The total mass distribution in galaxies strongly influences star formation (SF), both directly via gas availability and feedback, and indirectly via the dark matter (DM) potential well. Star formation efficiency peaks at a characteristic stellar mass of $5 \times 10^{10} M_{\odot}$, often termed the "golden mass", where feedback mechanisms minimally affect SF. This scale also appears in other scaling relations: correlations between stellar population gradients, DM fraction, and total mass density slope versus stellar mass all show pronounced minima at the golden mass. How, why, and when does this golden mass emerge during galaxy evolution? Early cosmic epochs ($z > 1.5$) remain largely unexplored, yet they probe the formative stages of galaxy mass assembly. They provide the full redshift baseline needed to constrain the buildup of stellar mass and the efficiency of gas-to-star conversion. By combining high resolution, multiplexing, and a wide field of view, SHARP on the ELT will address these questions, delivering sharper and deeper data than JWST. In this SHARP White Paper contribution, we present a scientific case exploiting these unique characteristics. SHARP will enable precise measurements of stellar population gradients, DM fractions, and total mass density slopes. Using the mIFU mode, SHARP will obtain spatially-resolved stellar population and dynamical maps for galaxies at $z > 1.5$. Reconstructing these observables for early- and late-type systems will allow us to trace the emergence of the golden mass across cosmic time, and to decode the impact of galaxy processes (mergers, SN, and AGN feedback) by comparing observations with cosmological simulations. (abridged)

</details>


### [124] [Supermassive black holes and nuclear star clusters in galaxies: constraining formation and co-evolution with SHARP and MICADO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06334)
*E. Dalla Bonta',E. Portaluri,C. Arcidiacono,E. Bortolas,S. Ciroi,E. M. Corsini,D. Gasparri,L. Morelli,A. Pizzella,M. Signorini*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了核星团（NSCs）与超大质量黑洞（SMBHs）在星系核中的共存与演化关系，提出利用ELT望远镜上SHARP光谱仪与MICADO成像仪联合开展近红外积分场观测，以统一框架定量检验核结构形成与演化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键科学问题包括：核星团的物理组装机制、超大质量黑洞在何种质量范围内主导动力学、以及致密恒星核被抑制、破坏或被核球状结构取代的条件。

Method: 结合ELT望远镜上的SHARP近红外积分场光谱仪与MICADO高分辨率成像仪，联合分析恒星动力学、恒星种群与核结构，实现黑洞质量、核星团内部运动学及气体流入特征的一致性测量。

Result: 明确了可获得稳健约束的黑洞质量与距离范围；揭示高红移致密活动核中黑洞可在弱/致密恒星成分系统中高效增长；本地多样核构型为研究黑洞与核恒星结构的耦合演化提供了实验室。

Conclusion: SHARP+MICADO将提供前所未有的解析能力，使本地星系成为检验早期宇宙核演化物理机制的关键实验室，推动对NSC-SMBH协同演化的定量理解。

Abstract: Nuclear star clusters and supermassive black holes are the dominant compact components in galaxy nuclei. They can coexist within the same system, and their relative prominence traces the history of gas inflow, star formation, and dynamical evolution on parsec scales. Key questions remain open concerning the physical channels that assemble nuclear star clusters, the mass regimes in which supermassive black holes become dynamically dominant, and the conditions under which compact stellar nuclei are suppressed, destroyed, or replaced by cusp-like structures. We describe how near-infrared integral-field spectroscopy with SHARP on the Extremely Large Telescope, combined with high-resolution imaging from MICADO, enables quantitative tests of nuclear formation and evolution scenarios within a unified framework. The joint analysis of stellar dynamics, stellar populations, and nuclear structure allows black-hole masses, internal kinematics of nuclear star clusters, and signatures of gas inflow to be measured consistently within the same systems, and defines the mass and distance ranges where robust constraints can be obtained. Recent discoveries of compact active nuclei at high redshift indicate that efficient black-hole growth can occur in systems with weak or compact stellar components. Although nearby galaxies are not evolutionary analogues of early systems, the diversity of nuclear configurations accessible with SHARP and MICADO provides a local laboratory for isolating the physical mechanisms that regulate the coupled evolution of central black holes and nuclear stellar structures.

</details>


### [125] [Net Cooling Tables for Cosmological Simulations under Multiple UV/X-ray Background Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06360)
*Laura Di Federico,Klaus Dolag,Ildar Khabibullin,Luca Sala,Geray S. Karademir,Milena Valentini*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提供了三套适用于宇宙学流体动力学模拟的净冷却表，基于CLOUDY 23.01计算，考虑了三种不同的紫外/软X射线背景模型；结果表明，不同背景模型在高红移（z≳4.6）下对冷却率影响显著，尤其在弥散气体中，甚至导致约10%的密度-温度参数空间内对净冷却/加热的判断相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冷却表在高红shift下对UV/X-ray背景依赖性缺乏系统评估，而该背景显著影响气体热演化，尤其在IGM中，亟需一致、可比的新冷却表。

Method: 使用CLOUDY 23.01对光学薄、处于光电离平衡的气体计算三组冷却/加热/净冷却率表；覆盖扩展的氦质量分数与13种重元素；采用元素分解方式，并兼容已有表格格式；输出包括H/He冷却加热率、离子丰度、电子丰度和平均粒子质量。

Result: 在z≳4.6时，不同背景模型导致冷却时间差异达一个数量级；在z≈8时，约10%的弥散气体（n_H–T平面）中，新旧表格对净冷却/加热的判断相反；该效应在纯H/He气体中依然存在。

Conclusion: 新冷却表为评估辐射背景对宇宙学模拟中气体冷却的影响提供了统一、可靠的基础，凸显了在高红移模拟中精确建模背景辐射的重要性。

Abstract: We present three sets of net cooling tables for cosmological hydrodynamical simulations, computed with CLOUDY 23.01 for optically thin gas in photoionization equilibrium with three different UV/X-ray background models. Following the element-by-element approach, and in a format compatible with existing tables, we tabulate cooling, heating, and net rates separately for hydrogen and helium over an extended range of helium mass fractions and for 13 heavy elements, together with hydrogen species fractions, electron abundances, and mean particle masses. For gas of Solar composition, the choice of UV/X-ray background has little impact on the net cooling rates at low redshift, but the results increasingly diverge towards high redshift, particularly in diffuse gas near thermal equilibrium. Compared with widely used previous tables, cooling times in this regime differ by up to an order of magnitude at $z \gtrsim 4.6$, and the calculations disagree on whether the gas undergoes net cooling or heating over nearly 10% of the diffuse density-temperature plane at $z \approx 8$, corresponding to the intergalactic medium. These trends persist for gas composed exclusively of hydrogen and helium. The tables provide a consistent framework for assessing the impact of the radiation background on gas cooling in cosmological simulations.

</details>


### [126] [AGN Host Galaxies in COSMOS-Web: Linking Structural Evolution and Star Formation Quenching up to $z=5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06443)
*Xiao Han,Lilan Yang,T. Kelly,Matthew A. Malkan,Luis C. Ho,John D. Silverman,Zi-Jian Li,Andreas L. Faisst,Andrew J. Battisti,Jeyhan S. Kartaltepe,Lauro Moscardini,Rasha M. Samir,Ronaldo Laishram,Tao Wang,Yiyang Zhang,Caitlin M. Casey,Henry Joy McCracken,Jason Rhodes,Brant E. Robertson,Maximilien Franco,Daizhong Liu,Marko Shuntov,Xuheng Ding*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST高分辨率成像和多波段测光数据，研究了0<z<5范围内3955个AGN宿主星系在尺寸-质量平面上的分布特征，并与恒星形成星系（SFGs）和宁静星系（QGs）对比，发现AGN宿主星系的结构演化路径存在质量与红移依赖性，部分可能经历‘先抑制恒星形成、后结构致密化’的非典型演化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活动星系核（AGN）在星系从恒星形成向宁静态转变过程中对恒星形成压制和结构演化的具体作用仍不明确，亟需通过大样本、高分辨观测厘清其宿主星系的结构-恒星形成关联。

Method: 基于五种多波段诊断方法选出3955个0<z<5的AGN；结合COSMOS-Web巡天的JWST高分辨率成像与归档多波段测光，构建AGN宿主星系的静止光学波段有效半径-恒星质量关系；与SFGs和QGs对比，并在Δlog SFR_MS–ΔΣ_e平面分析恒星形成活动与结构致密性的关联。

Result: 1）高红移（z≳2）大质量AGN宿主尺寸接近SFGs但恒星形成已受抑；低红移时逐渐靠近QG关系，伴随sSFR下降和Sérsic指数上升；低质量AGN宿主始终靠近SFG关系；2）AGN宿主整体尺寸演化较缓（R_e ∝ (1+z)^−0.6）；3）z<2.2时多数符合致密化致熄火模型，z>2.2时部分AGN宿主呈现‘恒星形成受抑但结构仍延展’的异常状态。

Conclusion: AGN宿主星系的演化并非单一路径：大质量宿主在高红移可能经历‘先熄火、后致密化’的替代机制，挑战了经典的致密化驱动AGN反馈致熄火模型，暗示AGN活动与星系结构演化之间存在更复杂的时序关系。

Abstract: The role of active galactic nuclei (AGNs) in the suppression of star formation and structural evolution of galaxies during their transition from star-forming to quiescent systems remains unclear. We analyze 3,955 AGNs selected through five multiwavelength diagnostics at $0<z<5$. Using high-resolution JWST imaging from the COSMOS-Web survey and archival multi-wavelength photometry, we investigate the rest-frame optical size-mass distribution of AGN host galaxies and compare it with those of star-forming galaxies (SFGs) and quiescent galaxies (QGs). We find that the positions of AGN hosts in the size-mass plane depend on both redshift and stellar mass. At $z\gtrsim2$, massive AGN hosts ($M_\star/M_\odot>10^{10.5}$) generally exhibit sizes comparable to those of SFGs despite suppressed star formation. Toward lower redshifts, they increasingly approach the QG size-mass relation, accompanied by declining specific star formation rates (sSFRs) and increasing Sérsic indices. In contrast, lower-mass AGN hosts remain close to the SFG relation across the redshift range. Overall, AGN hosts exhibit relatively mild size evolution, following $R_e\propto(1+z)^{-0.6}$ over $0<z<4$, shallower than that of typical SFGs and QGs. We further examine the relationship between star formation activity and structural compactness in the $Δ\log\mathrm{SFR}_{\mathrm{MS}}$-$ΔΣ_e$ plane. At $z<2.2$, AGN hosts predominantly occupy the compact star-forming and quiescent regions, broadly consistent with the compaction-quenching scenario. At $z>2.2$, however, some AGN hosts exhibit suppressed star formation while retaining relatively extended structures. These findings suggest that some AGN hosts may follow alternative evolutionary pathways in which star formation suppression precedes substantial structural compaction.

</details>


### [127] [Gaia DR3 Reveals the Kinematics of Young Stellar Objects in the Carina Nebula: Peripheral Expansion and Central Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06488)
*Xinyu Yang,Yuxin He,Chaojie Hao,Yiwei Dong,Y. Xu,Yingjie Li,ZeHao Lin,DeJian Liu,Jarken Esimbek,Jianjun Zhou,Gang Wu,Xindi Tang,Dalei Li*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究利用Gaia DR3、CO分子气体观测和APOGEE径向速度数据，分析了船底座星云中年轻恒星天体（YSOs）的运动学特征，发现外围区域存在系统性向外膨胀，中心区域呈现有序旋转，表明大质量恒星反馈主导该巨分子云的动力学演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究船底座星云中年轻恒星天体的运动学特征，以理解大质量恒星反馈对该巨分子云动力学演化的影响。

Method: 结合Gaia DR3的年轻恒星天体（YSOs）自行数据、$^{12}\mathrm{CO}\,(1\mbox{--}0)$分子气体观测以及APOGEE径向速度数据，分析不同子区域YSOs的运动学行为，并估算动量以探讨膨胀成因。

Result: 外围四个子区域YSOs表现出系统性向外膨胀，二维膨胀速度为0.57–4.56 km/s；中心区域YSOs呈现绕轴有序旋转，位置角为86.9±1.5°，旋转速度为1.75±0.03 km/s；膨胀可能源于大质量OB星反馈与可能的超新星爆发共同作用。

Conclusion: 大质量恒星反馈在船底座星云巨分子云的动力学演化中起主导作用。

Abstract: We investigate the kinematics of the Carina Nebula using a sample of young stellar objects (YSOs) cross-matched with Gaia DR3, together with $^{12}\mathrm{CO}\,(1\mbox{--}0)$ molecular gas observations and APOGEE radial-velocity data. The results show that the proper motions of the YSOs in the four peripheral subregions of Carina display a systematic outward expansion, and that the two-dimensional expansion velocities of the subregions are approximately $0.57\mbox{--}4.56~\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$. The YSOs in the Central Region exhibit ordered rotation around an axis at a position angle of $86.9\pm1.5^\circ$, with a rotation velocity of $1.75\pm0.03~\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$. Further momentum estimates suggest that the current expansion in the peripheral regions may result from the combined effects of feedback from massive OB stars and possible past supernova explosions. These results indicate that feedback from massive stars plays a dominant role in the dynamical evolution of the giant molecular cloud in the Carina Nebula.

</details>


### [128] [Primary and Secondary Star Formation in the Spiral Waves of NGC 628](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06516)
*Bruce G. Elmegreen,Sean T. Linden,Kelsey E. Johnson,Angela Adamo,Daniela Calzetti,Varun Bajaj,Giacomo Bortolini,Matteo Correnti,Ana Duarte-Cabral,Kathryn Grasha,Goran Ostlin,Alex Pedrini,Jenna Ryon,Linda J. Smith*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了NGC 628星系中旋臂结构与恒星反馈对恒星形成的影响，发现年轻星团在内区旋臂中分布更广，不同成因（密度波压缩 vs. 反馈压缩）的星团质量函数存在差异，且次级恒星形成削弱了旋臂内的年龄梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究旋臂结构（密度波）和恒星反馈在恒星形成中的相对作用，特别是如何影响星团的空间分布、年龄和质量函数。

Method: 基于NGC 628的7.7微米图像，将新兴年轻星团（eYSCs）按其位置归类为四类形态特征：旋臂细丝、旋臂侧枝、际臂羽状结构和气泡边缘；结合旋臂动力学模型（如共转半径）分析空间与年龄分布。

Result: 1）最年轻星团在内区旋臂中延展更远；2）旋臂细丝中的eYSCs（第一代）质量上限高于气泡边缘的eYSCs（第二代）；3）气泡相关eYSCs贡献约三分之一总质量；4）外旋臂中两类eYSCs混合更显著，可用于区分波状臂与物质臂并定位共转半径；5）17个最重星团中有8个组成4对紧密双星团，其中至少一对的动力学时标与年龄相当。

Conclusion: 密度波主导第一代恒星形成，而恒星反馈驱动第二代形成，二者协同塑造星团分布与质量函数；外旋臂的混合分布特征可作为识别波状旋臂及共转半径的重要观测判据。

Abstract: To determine the role of spiral arms and stellar feedback on the formation of stars, we examine the positions of optically identified and emerging star clusters in NGC 628. We find that the youngest clusters extend further into the spiral arms at small radii than at large radii, as expected when the angular flow rate through a wave-like arm increases with greater distance from corotation. The embedded or emerging star clusters (eYSCs) are identified relative to four distinct morphological features in the 7.7 micron image: spiral arm filaments, spiral arm spurs, spiral interarm feathers, and bubbles. Spiral arm-related eYSCs are presumably first-generation stars made from interstellar gas that was directly compressed by the density wave, while clusters along the rims of bubbles are secondary generations produced by feedback compression. The mass functions of the first and second generations differ: eYSCs in spiral arm filaments extend to significantly higher masses than eYSCs in bubbles. Secondary star formation continues downstream from primary star formation, removing the expected age gradient through the arms. Bubble eYSCs account for approximately one-third of all the emerging cluster mass. The outer arms have a more mixed distribution of spiral- and bubble-related eYSCs than the inner arms, potentially providing a way to distinguish wave and material arms and to find corotation in wave-like arms. Among the 17 most massive clusters with log M(Msun)>4, eight of them are in four tight pairs; in at least one case, the dynamical time for the pair is comparable to the cluster ages.

</details>


### [129] [The dwarf galaxy NGC 185: the flagship of Andromeda's dwarf galaxies for the study of evolved multiple stellar populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06547)
*Paolo Ventura,Chiara Ventura,Richard D'Souza,Flavia Dell'Agli,Matteo Correnti,Claudio Gavetti,Marco Tailo,Miriam Di Stefano,Eric Bell*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用水平支（HB）、红团簇（RC）及红巨星支（RGB）和渐近巨星支（AGB）恒星，结合恒星演化模型与星族合成模拟，对矮椭圆星系NGC 185开展恒星形成历史（SFH）重建，突破主序转折点（MSTO）方法的距离限制，适用于10–15 Mpc量级的星系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在HST和JWST观测下延伸对10–15 Mpc距离处矮椭圆星系的恒星形成历史诊断能力，需发展不依赖于微弱主序转折点（MSTO）的新方法。

Method: 基于低、中质量恒星演化模型，开展星族合成模拟，拟合NGC 185在（mF606W−mF814W, mF814W）颜色-星等图中的恒星分布与光度函数；利用HB恒星确定距离模数、消光、RGB阶段平均质量损失及古老（>7 Gyr）SFH；利用RC光度函数约束近期（<8 Gyr）SFH；结合HB形态与RGB斜率分析金属丰度演化。

Result: 得出NGC 185消光E(B−V)=0.20 mag、距离模数DM=24.10 mag；RGB阶段平均质量损失为0.22±0.01 M⊙；半数恒星形成于11 Gyr前；>8 Gyr恒星基本无金属丰度弥散，而<5–6 Gyr恒星略有增丰；确认在合理约束RGB质量损失前提下，演化恒星可提供可靠SFH。

Conclusion: 水平支、红团簇与红巨星支等演化阶段恒星可作为替代MSTO的有效SFH示踪体，显著拓展对中等距离矮椭圆星系的恒星考古能力，并为更远系统提供方法基础，但其精度仍受限于当前恒星演化模型的不确定性。

Abstract: Reaching galaxies at 10-15 Mpc with HST and JWST requires star
  formation history (SFH) diagnostics that do not rely on the faint
  main sequence turn-off. We investigate whether horizontal branch (HB),
  red clump (RC), and red and asymptotic giant branch (RGB, AGB) stars can
  fill this role for dwarf elliptical galaxies, using NGC 185 as a test case.
  We use stellar evolution models of low- and intermediate-mass stars as inputs
  to population synthesis simulations that reproduce the colour distribution and
  luminosity function of stars in the (mF606W-mF814W, mF814W)
  diagram. The study of the stars populating the HB is used to derive
  the distance modulus and reddening, the average mass loss suffered by the stars during
  the ascending on the RGB and the SFH occurred during the ancient epochs, before 7 Gyr ago.
  The luminosity function of the RC gives us constraints on the recent SFH (<8 Gyr).
  The distribution of HB stars yields a reddening E(B-V)=0.20 mag and a distance
  modulus DM=24.10 mag for NGC 185. The average mass lost by low-mass stars during
  the RGB phase is 0.22 +- 0.01 Msun. Star formation
  was concentrated at early epochs, with half of the stars formed more than 11 Gyr ago.
  The morphology of the HB indicates essentially no metallicity spread among stars older
  than 8 Gyr, whereas the slope of the RGB suggests that part of the stars younger than
  5-6 Gyr are slightly enriched in metals. Once RGB mass loss is adequately constrained, evolved stars can provide a
  reliable SFH for dwarf elliptical galaxies well beyond the reach of MSTO-based methods.
  We discuss the implications for more distant systems and the remaining uncertainties in
  the underlying stellar models.

</details>


### [130] [Living near filaments: How environment shapes age profiles in low-mass galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06632)
*Elisa A. Tau,Diego Pallero,Rory Smith,Raúl Baier-Soto,Antonela Monachesi,Silvio Varela-Lavin*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用IllustrisTNG-50模拟研究低质量星系中U型径向年龄分布的环境特征，发现这类星系多为中央星系、位于低质量暗晕中，且更靠近宇宙纤维结构而非星系群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释低质量星系中新近观测到的U型径向年龄分布（不同于常见的正梯度）所偏好的环境条件。

Method: 基于IllustrisTNG-50-50流体动力学模拟，选取恒星质量在10^9–10^10 M⊙之间的星系样本；定义U型年龄剖面为存在局部年龄极小值、两侧年龄差≥0.4 Gyr，并据此分类；统计其宿主晕质量、中心性及与星系群/宇宙纤维的距离。

Result: 11%（145个）星系呈现U型年龄剖面；其中72%为中心星系，82%位于M200 < 10^12 M⊙的低质量晕中；它们中位距离宇宙纤维约265 kpc，显著近于非U型星系（约351 kpc），且远离星系群。

Conclusion: U型年龄剖面在低质量星系中虽不占主导，但具有明确的环境偏好：倾向于孤立、低质量晕中的中央星系，且与宇宙大尺度纤维结构密切相关，暗示气体吸积或潮汐作用等外部过程可能驱动其恒星形成历史。

Abstract: U-shaped radial age profiles have recently been reported in low-mass galaxies. This type of profile differs from the usually observed positive radial age gradients of the low-mass regime. We aim to characterise the environment in which low-mass galaxies with U-shaped age profiles are more commonly found. We use the IllustrisTNG-50 hydrodynamical simulations to select a sample of galaxies with stellar masses ranging from $10^9 \, M_\odot$ to $10^{10} \, M_\odot$. Based on their radial age profiles, we classify the sample into galaxies with and without a U-shaped feature. We define a U-shaped profile as one where stellar ages decrease towards a local minimum and subsequently increase, requiring an age contrast of at least 0.4 Gyr on either side of the minimum relative to its value. We find 145 galaxies with U-shaped age profiles ($\sim 11\%$ of the sample). They are mostly central systems ($\sim 72\%$) and $\sim 82\%$ of them reside within low-mass halos ($M_{200} < 10^{12} \, M_\odot$). We also find that galaxies exhibiting this feature are located at large distances from galaxy groups but close to cosmic filaments, with a median distance of $\sim 265$ kpc, whereas those without U-shaped age profiles tend to be located farther away from these structures ($\sim 351$ kpc).

</details>


### [131] [Investigating the initial conditions of globular clusters via $N$-body simulations: I. half-mass radius and stellar density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06648)
*Ruoyun Huang,Baitian Tang,Long Wang,Holger Baumgardt,Xia Li,Genhao Liu,Jundan Nie,Hao Tian*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过高精度N体模拟研究了两个低质量外晕球状星团（Eridanus和Whiting 1）的长期动力学演化，反推出其初始半质量半径和表面密度远高于当前值，并指出高初始面密度和/或低金属丰度可能是多星族形成的必要条件；同时利用模拟结果识别出Whiting 1的五个高置信度潮汐尾候选体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解球状星团（GCs）的初始形成条件，特别是低质量外晕GCs为何呈现弥散结构、是否具有多星族（MPs），以及其与年轻大质量星团（YMCs）的异同。

Method: 使用高精度直接N体模拟代码PeTar对Eridanus和Whiting 1进行长期动力学演化模拟，并将模拟得到的数密度剖面与观测对比以约束初始半质量半径；结合Gaia DR3和APOGEE DR17数据识别潮汐尾成员。

Result: 得出Eridanus和Whiting 1的初始半质量半径分别为2.0±1.0 pc和2.5±0.5 pc，对应初始表面密度比当前高2–3个数量级；发现Whiting 1无多星族而Eridanus有，支持高初始面密度/低金属丰度利于MP形成；识别出5个Whiting 1的高概率额外潮汐候选体。

Conclusion: 低质量球状星团当前的弥散结构源于长期动力学膨胀与潮汐剥离，其原初状态显著致密；初始表面密度和金属丰度是决定是否形成多星族的关键物理参数。

Abstract: Born out of the turbulent, dense gas of the early Universe, globular clusters (GCs) represent a significantly different star-formation mode compared to the local Universe. To quantify the initial cluster conditions, we perform the long-term dynamical evolution of GCs using the high-precision direct N-body simulation code PeTar. As the first step in a series of studies, we investigate the two low-mass outer-halo GCs, Eridanus and Whiting 1. By comparing simulated number density profiles with observations, we derive best-fit initial half-mass radii of $2.0 \pm 1.0$ pc for Eridanus and $2.5 \pm 0.5$ pc for Whiting 1, corresponding to initial surface densities of $2592.03 M_\odot pc^{-2}$ and $226.35 M_\odot pc^{-2}$, respectively -- 2-3 orders of magnitude higher than their present-day values. The absence of multiple populations (MPs) in Whiting 1 and young massive clusters (YMCs), in contrast to the MP-hosting Eridanus, supports the scenario that MP formation requires higher initial surface density and/or lower metallicity. Using the simulations as a template, we identify five high-probability extra-tidal candidates of Whiting 1 from Gaia DR3 and APOGEE DR17, which exhibit coherent kinematics and chemical abundances. Our results demonstrate that the diffuse states of low-mass GCs are the product of long-term dynamical expansion and tidal stripping, and that their primordial configurations were significantly denser.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用公民科学家进行强引力透镜候选体分级（定级为“确定”、“可能”、“疑似”和“非透镜”）的有效性，并将其与专业研究人员的判断进行对比，发现志愿者经校准后可达到与专家相近的判别能力，但对中间等级样本存在显著主观分歧；研究建议未来透镜搜索应采用校准后的透镜概率以提升可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引力透镜样本发布前需进行人工分级，通常由少数研究人员完成，成本高、效率低且存在主观性；本文旨在探索公民科学是否能高效、可靠地承担该任务，并优化未来大规模巡天（如Euclid）中的透镜识别流程。

Method: 通过Zooniverse平台组织公民科学家对强引力透镜候选体进行四等级（definite/probable/possible/non-lens）分类；将志愿者响应与专业研究人员的标注对比，计算等级后验概率，并评估志愿者响应的收敛性与等效性（如1名研究员 ≈ 1.9名志愿者）；最后模拟在Euclid DR1中应用该流程的预期性能。

Result: 志愿者能准确识别各等级系统，其响应经校准后可与研究人员结果相当；非透镜系统等级后验概率收敛最快，而中间等级（probable/possible）在志愿者与研究人员间均存在显著分歧；若在Q1搜索中用志愿者替代研究员进行分级并校准，可在研究人员复核前实现79%的A+B级完备率和41%纯度，预计Euclid DR1中可获得约12500个真实透镜。

Conclusion: 公民科学家可用于强引力透镜的分级任务，尤其适用于大规模数据预筛；但需校准以弥合主观差异，未来应转向基于概率的透镜识别框架，以更好处理分级不确定性。

Abstract: We demonstrate the efficacy of crowd-sourced refinement of strong gravitational lens candidate samples by citizen scientists. Binary (lens/non-lens) classification by citizen scientists has been conducted previously, and here we extend such categorisation into `definite', `probable', `possible', and `non-lenses'. This grading is a required stage ahead of any strong lens sample publication and is usually carried out by (small) teams of researchers. We compare the responses of the volunteers to those of strong lens researchers and discuss the optimal strategy for lens classification and grading in future strong lens searches. Volunteers are found to be able to correctly identify systems of each grade, and their responses can be calibrated to be comparable to those of researchers. Grade posteriors, defined as the probability that a strong lens system classified by the volunteers is a certain grade, converge most rapidly for non-lens systems. We find that one researcher classification is equivalent to only 1.9 volunteer classifications when grading such systems. There is significant disagreement in the grading of intermediate-grade lens candidates amongst both volunteers and researchers. Moving towards calibrated lens probabilities for future lens searches would help to account for such subjectivity. If the Q1 lens search was rerun with volunteers performing lens grading (and corresponding calibration), a completeness of 79% for Grades A+B (corresponding to definite + probable lenses respectively) could be achieved with 41% purity prior to researcher inspection - this would correspond to an expected 12500 true lenses (i.e. excluding contaminants) in Euclid DR1.

</details>


### [133] [Magnetic Properties of Galactic Molecular Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06792)
*Philip C. Myers,Ian W. Stephens,Simon Coudé,Nicole Karnath,Howard A. Smith*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过拟合CO巡天观测的约1500个分子云的质量-半径关系，发现其符合磁亚临界、压强束缚的维里平衡模型，推断出银河系内巨分子云普遍具有较弱但非可忽略的磁场（Btot≈60μG），并由此解释其低恒星形成效率（SFE≈0.5–2%）的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释近年观测到的分子云质量-半径关系指数s介于2–3之间（偏离经典Larson第三关系），并探究其物理成因，尤其是磁场与压强在维里平衡中的作用。

Method: 将磁亚临界云的维里平衡（VE）方程拟合到多个CO巡天观测（包括银河系及M31、M33）中约1500个分子云的M–R数据；结合极化、Zeeman、DCF观测和MHD模拟进行交叉验证。

Result: 拟合得到银河系内分子云典型参数：质量-磁通比λ≈0.7（亚临界）、总磁场Btot≈60μG、中面压强Pe/k≈5×10⁵ cm⁻³ K；‘骨骼’结构也支持亚临界结论；MHD模拟与观测一致地给出SFE≈0.5–2%。

Conclusion: 多数巨分子云处于压强束缚、磁亚临界的维里平衡态；其较强大尺度磁场抑制快速坍缩，导致低恒星形成效率；而小尺度（团块/核）磁场已弱至允许引力坍缩。

Abstract: The earliest studies of molecular clouds (MCs) showed scaling relations between mass and radius M$\sim$R^s with exponent s $\approx$ 2, known as the third Larson relation. More recent CO surveys indicate 2 < s < 3 in the inner Milky Way (MW) and nearby galaxies. These observations appear consistent with the virial equilibrium (VE) of magnetically subcritical clouds, bound by the pressure Pe of the midplane ISM. The VE equations for such clouds are consistent with 2 < s < 3 for magnetically subcritical clouds, but not for magnetically critical, supercritical, or nonmagnetic clouds. We fit the subcritical VE equations to M-R trends observed in CO surveys of $\sim$1500 clouds, with correlation coefficients 0.8 - 1. The fits indicate median mass-to-flux ratio lambda $\approx$ 0.7, magnetic field strength Btot $\approx$ 60$\mathrm{μG}$, and Pe/k $\approx$ 5 x 10^5 cm^-3 K in the inner MW, with lesser values in M33 and M31. For 18 MW "bones," VE analysis assuming likely values of Pe also indicates subcritical values of lambda, as found from polarization observations. These results indicate that many giant molecular clouds (GMCs) are consistent with pressure-bound magnetically subcritical virial equilibrium. If so, GMCs may have low star formation efficiency (SFE) because their relatively strong fields cause filaments, clumps and cores to form slowly, while feedback and turbulence disperse them rapidly. The fields in their clumps and cores are likely weak enough to allow star-forming collapse, according to Zeeman and DCF observations, and according to MHD simulations which predict SFE $\approx$ 0.5-2%.

</details>


### [134] [ReveaLLAGN 3: Revealing the Infrared Spectral Energy Distributions of Low Luminosity AGN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06916)
*David Ohlson,Anil C. Seth,Kameron Goold,Nischal Acharya,Torsten Böker,Antoine Dumont,Ruby Duncan,Michael Eracleous,Anja Feldmeier-Krause,Juan Antonio Fernández-Ontiveros,Elena Gallo,Andy D. Goulding,Jenny E. Greene,Luis C. Ho,Sera B. Markoff,Mallory Molina,Richard M. Plotkin,Almudena Prieto,Shobita Satyapal,Glenn van de Ven,Jonelle L. Walsh*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）观测七个近邻低光度活动星系核（AGN）的1.7–24 μm红外谱能量分布，通过二维图像空间拟合分离出干净的、无宿主星系污染的AGN本征光谱；发现其近红外辐射相对增强、中红外谱斜率更陡、硅酸盐发射强，且与射电噪度相关，挑战现有低光度AGN理论模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光度AGN的红外谱能量分布（SED）缺乏高分辨率、无星系污染的观测约束，现有理论模型难以解释其物理机制，亟需JWST等新设备提供清洁光谱进行检验。

Method: 利用JWST在1.7–24 μm波段对七个近邻低光度AGN进行成像光谱观测；采用二维空间拟合方法从延展星系光中分离出点源AGN成分；对比孔径光谱与空间拟合光谱以评估星系污染程度；结合X射线数据开展多波段比值分析，并量化近/中红外谱斜率与颜色。

Result: 1）成功获得无星系污染的AGN本征红外光谱；2）低光度AGN近红外/X射线比增强，且与射电噪度正相关；3）多数目标呈现强致密硅酸盐发射，表明存在秒差距尺度暖尘；4）中红外谱斜率更陡、颜色更红，而近红外谱形与高光度AGN相似。

Conclusion: 低光度AGN的红外SED特征（强硅酸盐发射、陡峭中红外谱）与当前主流模型预测不符，暗示其尘埃结构、辐射机制或吸积物理可能与高光度AGN存在本质差异，需发展新模型。

Abstract: The spectral energy distributions of active galactic nuclei (AGN) provide valuable information on the emission mechanisms taking place in their inner regions. Here we use the James Webb Space Telescope to study the near and mid-infrared spectral energy distributions of seven nearby low-luminosity active galaxy nuclei from 1.7 to 24 $μm$. We cleanly separate the AGN spectrum by performing spatial fits of the two-dimensional images across all wavelengths. We then compare these clean unresolved spectra to aperture-based spectra and show that they effectively remove galaxy contamination. Comparing our infrared measurements to X-ray emission, we find that low-luminosity AGN have enhanced near-infrared emission relative to more luminous AGN, a trend that correlates with radio-loudness, while their mid-infrared-to-X-ray ratios are comparable. A majority of our targets show strong compact silicate emission suggesting the presence of warm dust on parsec scales in these systems. We also quantify the near- and mid-infrared power-law slopes and colors of low-luminosity AGN. In the near-infrared we find AGN of all luminosities have similar spectral slopes and colors, however in the mid-infrared, we find low-luminosity AGN typically have steeper slopes and redder colors than more luminous AGN. Both the strong silicate emission and steep mid-infrared slopes disagree with existing models for low-luminosity AGN spectral energy distributions.

</details>
